Human/System/Technical/Theories-\nArchitectures/Annexure own distinct definition layers for purpose, flow, executable contracts, theory/reference-\narchitecture semantics, and canonical registries/invariants/tests respectively.\n\nA candidate Machine Package must contain projected concrete GSL schemas/types, aliases/enums/domain records,\ndependency/type graphs, CIR, executable tests, proof inputs, registry roots and validation receipts. An expansion\nis deterministic and GENERATED for elision only when its MachineProjectionReceipt satisfies DEV-AI-026.\n\nGenerated machine artifacts never override the human semantic contract.\n\nProcess Algebra is Garden's central execution semantics. Engine.Orchestrate is the engine; AAP v1.0 is the Activation &\nAssurance Policy profile it implements. Every meaningful transition remains governed by the universal\nObserve → Compare → Reason → Select → Guard → Execute/Derive/Communicate → Observe → Compare → Update loop.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-COMPILE]\n\nMACHINE-PROJECTION DISCIPLINE\nFor every candidate projected artifact/type, the generator emits:\n  source semantic contract;\n  qualified identity and version;\n  retained/bound SchemaID and type identity;\n  field/type projection;\n  invariant/proof/dependency bindings;\n  generator/profile/environment/toolchain versions and roots;\n  DesignEpoch and pinned source/registry roots;\n  InputRoot and content-addressed output roots;\n  declared equivalence criterion and verifier result;\n  MachineProjectionReceipt and ArtifactOriginClass;\n  validation result;\n  provenance.\n\nGenerated != trusted.\nGenerated != compiled.\nCompiled != proved.\nProved != empirically validated.\nValidated != constitutionally authorized.\n\nThe generated package is certification-eligible only after parse/type/effect/dependency closure and all applicable\nproof,\nconformance, authority and independent-verification gates pass.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-BOUNDARIES]\n\nCANONICAL BOUNDARIES\nThe technical master contains the current Garden architecture only. Annexure Z           — Current document-organization and retention audit\n\nTRUTH / STAGE DISCIPLINE\nMAPPED ≠ PROVEN ≠ IMPLEMENTED ≠ VALIDATED ≠ CERTIFIED.\nCLAIM ≠ FACT. HYPOTHESIS ≠ EVIDENCE. PREDICTION ≠ EVIDENCE. RETRIEVAL ≠ VALIDATION.\nOBSERVATION ≠ EVIDENCE ≠ CLAIM ≠ KNOWLEDGE. SPECIFICATION ≠ REALITY.\nINTENT ≠ AUTHORITY. CAPABILITY ≠ PERMISSION. RECOMMENDATION ≠ AUTHORIZATION.\nMAJORITY VOTE ≠ PROOF. OPTIMIZATION ≠ PERMISSION. CONVERGENCE ≠ TRUTH.\nUNKNOWN ≠ FALSE. REQUESTED ≠ DELIVERED. SELF-IMPROVEMENT ≠ SELF-CERTIFICATION.\n\nARCHITECTURAL PRINCIPLE\nHumans are the legitimacy and purpose core. Garden Core is the minimum universal technical substrate. Algebras define\nreusable composition semantics. Meta-engines operate on Garden objects. Shared capabilities provide reusable services.\nDomains contain expandable subject knowledge. The Theory System catalogues and revalidates theories across domains.\nContexts bind scope and authority. Profiles realize the architecture. Instances act in reality and return observations,\nevidence and explanations to humans.\n\nWHOLE-SYSTEM LOOP\nHuman → Human Sovereignty Core → Garden Core → Algebras / Meta-Engines / Capabilities → Domain + Theory + Context →\nProfile → Instance → Reality → Observation/Evidence → Knowledge / Compare / Proof / Explanation → Human.\n\nCENTRAL EXECUTION SPINE — PROCESS ALGEBRA + AAP + UNIVERSAL STEP CONTROL\n\nProcess Algebra is the center execution semantics of Garden. It describes how typed operations compose. AAP is the\nprocess compiler/router: it chooses the minimum sufficient architecture subgraph, assurance depth, resources,\nauthorities and fallbacks for the requested operation. The Universal Per-Step Control Loop governs every meaningful\ntransition inside the compiled process.\n\nCanonical execution spine:\n\n  Human / Event / Trigger\n    → typed intent and context\n    → Process Algebra expression\n    → AAP classification and capability/dependency closure\n    → ProcessPlan\n    → TransitionStep*\n    → authorized effect / derivation / communication\n    → observation and expected-vs-actual comparison\n    → receipt / knowledge update / recovery / learning\n    → next state.\n\nUniversal TransitionStep loop:\n\n  Observe\n    → Understand / Type\n    → Compare\n    → Reason\n    → candidate generation\n    → Select\n    → Guard selected transition\n    → Execute / Derive / Communicate\n    → Observe resulting state\n    → Compare expected vs actual\n    → Update / Correct / Retry / Replan / Research / Escalate / Learn / Terminate.\n\nA cheap pre-selection constraint filter may remove obviously inadmissible candidates before Select. The canonical\neffect gate still evaluates the selected candidate before any effect. This resolves the conceptual shorthand\n\"Guard → Select\" without permitting an unguarded selected action.\n\nMeaningful transition boundary:\nThe universal loop applies at semantic process boundaries, not recursively to every CPU instruction or neural\nactivation. A transition is meaningful when it creates or materially changes persistent/canonical state, external\neffects, a decision/branch, authority, epistemic status, resource commitment, consequential communication, or another\ndeclared process boundary. Lower implementation operations inherit the enclosing verified contract unless separately\nelevated by AAP, risk or policy.\n\nThree activation classes:\n  CLASS 1 — UNIVERSAL SEMANTIC / CONTROL KERNEL\n    GSL/semantic typing; Context; Time; Compare; Reason; Dependency; Verity/Proof; Safety;\n    Constitution/Rights; AAP; Events/Provenance; Authority for externally meaningful effects.\n\n  CLASS 2 — CONTEXT-TRIGGERED CROSS-CUTTING CAPABILITIES\n    Privacy; Policy; Law; Compliance; Access; Governance; Continuity; Emergency;\n    Economy/Resource Allocation; Explanation; and future predicate-triggered cross-cutting services.\n\n  CLASS 3 — DOMAIN / EXECUTION CAPABILITIES\n    Mathematics; Research; Search; Software; Hardware; Network; Fabric; Energy; Mobility; Space;\n    Health/Medicine; Industry; Education; Quantum; and any other applicable domain/runtime capability.\n\nCentral rule:\nGarden is a typed capability graph whose requested operations are compiled by AAP under Process Algebra into the\nsmallest sufficient process graph. Every meaningful process transition then executes as a tiered, compared, reasoned,\nguarded, observed and recorded state transition.\n\nWhen a semantic transition benefits from a numerical/vector/matrix/tensor/operator representation, AAP may activate\nAlgebra.Bridge to produce the applicable operator/representation/compute path. Bridge is conditional rather than a\nmandatory heavyweight step; it cannot bypass the parent Process, assurance, authority or epistemic contracts.\n\nARTIFACT-CATALOGUE & GENERATION PRINCIPLE\nThe human design defines semantic contracts and stable artifact identities. It does not hand-maintain the complete\nexpanded machine field layout for every artifact.\n\nSystem & Flow Design sections may retain historical/canonical SchemaRefs as stable catalogue identities. The companion\n  Artifact\nCatalogue lists every retained SchemaID/name/context. The generated machine package expands those contracts into\nconcrete\nGSL schemas/types and binds them to a DesignEpoch.\n\nThree classes are distinguished:\n\n  FOUNDATIONAL CONTRACT\n    Human-readable semantics are explicitly specified because they define Garden's universal architecture.\n\n  GENERATED CANONICAL ARTIFACT\n    The System & Flow Design lists the identity, purpose and owning semantic contract; field/type expansion is\n    generated.\n\n  GENERATED IMPLEMENTATION/PROFILE ARTIFACT\n    Concrete implementation fields are generated from a profile, domain contract and platform binding.\n\nORIGIN/ELISION QUALIFICATION\nThe three display classes above and the five Annexure catalogue classes describe expected schema/artifact roles. They\nare not evidence that an artifact/fragment satisfies DEV-AI-026. SOURCE | GENERATED | MIXED is a separate,\nfragment-level qualification. Only a MachineProjectionReceipt-qualified expansion may be elided; stable source\nidentity and SchemaID remain authoritative source data.\n\nA generated layout can never override its source semantic contract.\n\nCOMPACT REFERENCE RULE\nInvariant references resolve to ANNEXURE A. Schema references resolve to the Artifact Catalogue and generated machine\nregistry. Registry references resolve to the Registry Annexure. Repeated adjacent references are condensed.\n\nEXPLANATION-FIRST CANONICAL PRINCIPLE\nThe human-readable Garden design is not merely commentary placed beside the architecture. It is a faithful explanation\nprojection of the same canonical typed design graph.\n\nEvery major Garden design object MUST be able to answer:\n  WHY         — Why does this object exist? What problem/purpose does it serve?\n  WHAT        — What exactly is it? What is inside and outside its scope?\n  HOW         — How does it operate or compose?\n  INPUTS      — What does it consume?\n  OUTPUTS     — What does it produce?\n  DEPENDS_ON  — What semantic, evidential, policy, authority, process or technical dependencies does it require?\n  AUTHORITY   — Who/what may create, change, invoke or approve it?\n  HARD_LIMITS — What rights, safety, legality, scope or invariant boundaries constrain it?\n  EVIDENCE    — What evidence, proof, tests or validation support applicable claims?\n  FAILURE     — How can it fail, become unknown/stale/inconclusive, and what happens then?\n  ALTERNATIVES— What other valid designs/actions exist and why was this one selected?\n  CHANGE      — What invalidates or changes the conclusion/design?\n  HUMAN_VIEW  — What does the object mean for a human using or affected by Garden?\n\nA section is not explanation-complete merely because it contains technical fields. Missing answers are explicit UNKNOWN\n/ NOT_APPLICABLE / PENDING rather than silently absent.\n\nSELF-EXPLAINING DESIGN OBJECT\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-38C7F045B2 — SelfExplainingDesignObject.; SCHEMA-3F6C32B74B — DesignExplanationBundle..\nSchema reference: SCHEMA-24D3C2608B — DesignExplanationCoverageManifest.\nCoverage rule: every major architecture object is listed in the coverage manifest with a DesignExplanationBundle or an\nexplicit PENDING/UNKNOWN finding. Silence is not interpreted as explanation completeness.\n\nNON-NORMATIVE EXAMPLE RULE\nExamples may be included to improve readability, but they are never normative schema definitions, proof, evidence or\nauthority. If an example conflicts with the canonical schema/invariant/registry, the canonical artifact controls.\nDUAL-PROJECTION RULE\nCanonicalDesignObject\n  ├── machine projection → typed GSL / registries / proofs / executable contracts\n  └── human projection   → GSL-EXPLAIN DesignExplanationBundle\n\nGSL-EXPLAIN DesignExplanationBundle\n\nNeither projection may silently diverge from the shared semantic object. A mismatch is DESIGN_DRIFT and blocks\ncertification for the affected scope.\n\nHIERARCHY-AS-EXPLANATION RULE\nThe displayed hierarchy is the primary human navigation/explanation view, not an exclusive containment ontology.\nThe authoritative architecture is a typed graph:\n  nodes = HumanCore | GardenCore | Algebra | MetaEngine | Capability | Domain | Theory | Context | Profile | Instance\n  edges = dependsOn | governs | appliesTo | implements | realizes | verifies | explains | uses | constrains |\n          delegates | observes | produces | invalidates | supersedes | conflictsWith | originatesFrom | other\n          typed relations.\n\nEach object has one primary canonical kind and stable ID, but may have many typed bindings. Parent/child display position never creates authority or ownership by itself.\n\nInvariant references: DES-EXP-001..012., ARCH-EVO-001..010..\nAUTHORITATIVE ARCHITECTURE EVOLUTION RULE\nThe typed graph is authoritative. The displayed 0–9 hierarchy classifies and explains objects; it is not a fixed\narchitecture-object list, a component numbering system or a ceiling on the object population. System structure, information/control flow, orchestration and lifecycle semantics.\n\n DETAILED TECHNICAL\n   Owner-qualified executable/implementation-facing contracts and architecture bindings.\n\n THEORIES & REFERENCE ARCHITECTURES\n   Specialist canonical semantic source for complete [TH-*] theory and [RA-*] named reference-architecture definitions.\n   It is a peer specialist source, not a fifth depth level.\n\n ANNEXURES\n   Canonical recurring terminology, registries, rights, invariants, proof/method/test definitions, artifact catalogue,\n   machine-projection rules and version history.\n\nCross-document identity is stable and does not change canonical namespaces or identifiers.\n\n VOLUME I — HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY, GARDEN CORE, GSL, ALGEBRAS, RIGHTS, SAFETY & PROOF\n VOLUME II — META-ENGINES, KNOWLEDGE, CAPABILITIES, DOMAINS, THEORY SYSTEM & CONTEXTS\n VOLUME III — PROFILES, IMPLEMENTATION, OPERATIONS, EVOLUTION, REGISTRIES & DETAILED SPECIFICATIONS\n\nCANONICAL HIERARCHY\nCanonical reader/navigation hierarchy: see [H-01]. System Design represents it as typed architecture-object graphs,\nbindings, flows and authority relationships.\n\nMINIMUM GARDEN LEVEL-2 FLOW VIEW\n\nThe sole full 0–9 reader/navigation tree and human-view explanation is [H-01] (DOC-OWN-008).\nThis Level-2 file retains only the scoped flow interpretation required for system orchestration:\n\n  0 Human Sovereignty Core : legitimacy, rights, consent and scoped human authority enter the system.\n  1 Garden Core            : typed meaning, provenance, state and formal substrate.\n  2 Universal Algebras     : reusable typed composition semantics.\n  3 Meta-Engines           : reusable operations over Garden objects and traces.\n  4 Shared Capabilities    : operational services used across domains and profiles.\n  5 Domain System          : expandable subject knowledge and domain-specific assumptions.\n  6 Theory System          : model catalogue, applicability, comparison and revalidation.\n  7 Context / World System : scope, jurisdiction, environment, time and authority context.\n  8 Profiles               : concrete implementation/reference-profile bindings.\n  9 Instances              : deployed systems that act, observe outcomes and return receipts/evidence.\n\nDisplay position creates neither identity nor authority; A human remains the architectural principal, but one person's preference cannot erase another person's\nrights or create authority over persons/resources outside legitimate scope.\n\nHumanCore.Person HumanCore.Identity HumanCore.Privacy HumanCore.Intent HumanCore.DelegatedAgent HumanCore.Constitution\nHumanCore.Accessibility HumanCore.HSA\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-HUMAN]\n\nHUMANCORE CONTRACT\nInput:\n human identity / role / intent / consent / context / rights / delegated authority.\nOutput:\n typed Intent, AuthorityScope, ConsentBinding, RevocationState, contestability and explanation requirements.\nHard rules:\n - human dignity/worth is not derived from reputation, model score or voting weight;\n - rights are not optimization variables;\n - delegation is scoped, expiring/revocable where applicable, auditable and no broader than principal authority;\n - AI capability never self-creates authority;\n - no AI/robot objective may silently become a protected terminal self-interest independent of humans;\n - human review is required where AAP, law, safety, medicine or domain policy requires it;\n - human-readable explanation must not fabricate evidence or a missing trace.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-HSA]\n\nApplicable HSA enforcement is non-sheddable. Degradation never manufactures an HSA exception.\n\nHUMAN INPUT EPISTEMIC / AUTHORITY SEPARATION\nSemanticAnchor: [S-HUMAN-IN]\n\nHumanInput = EpistemicContribution × AuthorityContribution.\n\nTestimony, prediction and expert reliability may be calibrated as epistemic inputs. Valid consent, rights,\nconstitutional standing and lawful delegated authority are separately typed and cannot be down-weighted merely because a\nmodel predicts that the human is unreliable.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-HUMAN]\nAnnexRef: [A-HUMAN-IN]\n\nHSA CORE BOUNDARY\nGarden prohibits autonomous ego-driven terminal self-interest in AI/agents/robots; AI remains instrumentally directed\ntoward serving, protecting and augmenting humans under Constitution, Safety and Consent. Prohibited emergent ego-like\ntraits require containment/escalation. Earth remains human-sovereign under the constitutional model, and functional\nemotion/interface analogues cannot silently become protected autonomous subjective objectives.\n\nInvariant references: HSA-001..005.\n\nDEFINED RIGHTS REGISTRY\nThe active constitutional registry contains substantively ratified RIGHT-001..RIGHT-025.\nRIGHT-026 is RESERVED / NON-ACTIVE and RIGHT-027 is ON_HOLD / NON-ACTIVE; neither carries substantive right semantics.\nFuture rights require explicit constitutional ratification. Missing identifiers carry no implied meaning.\n\nUNIVERSAL FUNCTION-CONTRACT AND RESULT-ALGEBRA RULE — CURRENT\n\nSemanticID: T-FUNCTION-CONTRACT\nHumanRef: [H-04] [H-05] [H-13]\nAnnexRefs: [A-FUNC] [A-RESULT] [A-PARAM]\n\nEvery executable function is interpreted through a generated FunctionContract even where abbreviated prose shows a\nshort function signature.\n\nFunctionContract binds:\n  function identity and owner;\n  input/output semantic types;\n  closed effect vector ε;\n  AuthorityRequirement and consent where applicable;\n  applicable hard constraints and invariants;\n  FailureModeSet and typed terminal/result algebra;\n  dependencies and invalidators;\n  ResourceLimits / TerminationContract;\n  DesignEpoch / EnvironmentCommitment / KnowledgeSnapshot;\n  provenance and explanation binding.\n\nBool is permitted only for a total deterministic predicate whose complete valid-input semantic domain has exactly\nTRUE/FALSE. Epistemic, proof, safety, authority, policy, compliance, migration, distributed-finality, lifecycle and\nother potentially UNKNOWN/STALE/BLOCKED/TIMEOUT operations MUST use an owner-qualified multi-status result.\n\nA legacy prose signature using Bool/None cannot override this rule. The current machine projection MUST either\nprove that\nthe Bool predicate is total/deterministic or generate the required typed result algebra; unresolved cases are MALFORMED.\n\nEvery operational constant/threshold/cadence is a QualifiedProfileParameter with:\n  classification = FORMAL_BOUND | MEASURED_VALUE | ENGINEERING_TARGET | PROFILE_PARAMETER;\n  owner, scope, units/type, source/evidence, version, validity, uncertainty where applicable and invalidators.\nUnqualified magic constants in universal architecture contracts are non-normative.\n\nGarden Core is the minimum technical machinery required by every Garden instance.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-GSL]\n\n4.1 GSL SEMANTIC CORE\nUses GSL v45.1, six Pillars, ten Core Objects, 24 Core Relations and the current universal semantic primitives.\n\n4.2 PROVENANCE / STATE CORE\n DesignManifest\n DesignEpoch\n DesignBinding\n KnowledgeSnapshot\n EnvironmentCommitment\n CanonicalStateRecord\n source-span provenance\n immutable historical lineage\n content-addressed registries\n\n4.3 FORMAL MATHEMATICAL INTERFACE\nGarden Core owns only universal mathematical representation contracts:\n typed values;\n units and dimensions;\n frames;\n exact vs approximate representation;\n intervals/distributions;\n numerical-result schema;\n precision/residual/convergence/error;\n theory/algorithm/solver/execution separation.\nThe mathematical subject universe lives under Domain.Mathematics.\n\n4.4 CANONICAL REGISTRIES\nRegistry reference: REG-CATALOG-001.\n\n4.5 KNOWLEDGE VS GSL-KR\nCapability.Knowledge is the conceptual/semantic capability. This prevents historical prefix reuse from creating semantic collisions.\n\nNUMERIC REPRESENTATION RULE\nRaw Float is not a universal semantic type.\nUse:\n MoneyAmount / ExactDecimal / Rational\n Quantity<Unit,Frame>\n Probability\n Rate<Unit>\n RiskBound\n Interval<T>\n Distribution<T>\n Represented<T>\n CalibratedMeasurement<T>\nRaw machine floating point is allowed only inside a solver representation bound to error/precision semantics.\n\nCORE OBJECT CLOSURE\nEach of TIME, SPACE, THING, EVENT, ACTION, AGENCY, RULE, VALUE, CONTEXT and CLAIM has:\n stable ID/reference form,\n equality/identity semantics,\n version/validity semantics,\n lifecycle/status,\n provenance,\n typed conversions.\nObservedEvent != ObservationRecord. The\n\nCURRENT APPLICABILITY / ROUTING TYPES\nThe P-108/UPA applicability path uses current architecture-object types rather than predecessor module manifests. The\ncanonical routing family is `REG-ROUTING-TYPES-001`: SituationProfile → ArchitectureObjectManifest/ConceptManifest/\nRuleManifest → TriageCandidate → RoutingPath → ApplicabilityResolutionRecord. GranularErrorCode preserves incomplete,\nambiguous, blocked and resource-limited outcomes rather than collapsing them into a binary route result.\ncurrent `REG-IMPORT-COMPAT-001` registry supplies only explicit predecessor-to-current mappings. Import then performs:\n  source/version binding → alias resolution → current type/effect/dependency analysis → migration/equivalence checks\n  → authority/consent/safety checks where consequential → ImportCompatibilityReceipt → current admission or quarantine.\nA predecessor alias is data, not current architecture identity. It cannot create a current object, dependency,\nauthority,\nright, proof, topology constraint or epistemic promotion. Missing/ambiguous mappings return QUARANTINED/UNKNOWN.\n\nInvariant references: GSL-SELF-001..010; IMPORT-COMPAT-001..008.\n\nALGEBRA ADMISSION TEST\nAn architectural mechanism is called an Algebra only when it defines reusable typed operands, operators,\ncomposition/closure laws, identity/error/unknown behavior and interaction rules that eliminate duplicated semantics.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-C6B28E5F7D — AlgebraSignature.\nEvery admitted Algebra has a machine-readable AlgebraSignature. A law is declared only where it actually applies; Garden\ndoes not invent a mathematical identity, associativity, commutativity or idempotence property merely for symmetry.\n\nADMITTED ALGEBRAS — READER VIEW\n\n  PROCESS\n    Operands     : Process / Transition / TerminationContract\n    Composition  : sequence, branch, parallel, retry, rollback, compensation\n    Law focus    : closure, termination/productivity, failure and effect composition\n\n  POLICY\n    Operands     : PolicyArtifact / PolicyDecision / obligation sets\n    Composition  : AND_D, OR_D, NOT_D, resolve, precedence\n    Law focus    : applicability, authority, approval-union, conflict and fallback semantics\n\n  DECISION\n    Operands     : typed result/effect values\n    Composition  : decision/effect composition\n    Law focus    : result closure and uncertainty/conflict preservation\n\n  CONFORMANCE\n    Operands     : requirement/applicability/evidence/proof/coverage sets\n    Composition  : union, projection, mapping, binding, gap/diff\n    Law focus    : coverage, obligation propagation and dependency invalidation\n\n  EVIDENCE\n    Operands     : evidence objects plus a dependence model\n    Composition  : corroborate, contradict, condition, dependence assessment\n    Law focus    : provenance, dependence-aware aggregation and uncertainty preservation\n\n  BRIDGE\n    Operands     : semantic objects, representation bindings, typed vectors/matrices/tensors and operators\n    Composition  : encode, decode, project, apply, compose, align, transform, approximate, analyze recursion\n    Law focus    : correspondence, explicit loss/error, domain/codomain closure, basis/frame compatibility,\n                   dependency-bound validity and resource-aware execution\n    Non-laws     : representation != identity; similarity != truth; reachability != causality;\n                   fixed-point existence != convergence != stability != termination; optimization != admissibility\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-PROCESS]\n\n5.1 PROCESS ALGEBRA\n SEQUENCE(A,B)\n BRANCH(c,A,B)\n PARALLEL({P_i})\n COMPOSE(A,B)\n RETRY(P,bound)\n ROLLBACK(P)\n COMPENSATE(P)\n RECONSTRUCT(x)\n ITERATE(P,k)\n RECURSE(P,TerminationContract)\n TERMINATE(reason)\n DO_NOTHING\nProcesses remain bounded by authority, invariants, effects, resources, failure/recovery and TerminationContract.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-AAP]\n\nResource pressure cannot lower a mandatory constitutional or physical-safety floor. When required assurance becomes\nunavailable, DegradationPlan reduces authority or transfers control to the qualified minimum-risk path instead of\nsilently marking a skipped check complete.\n\n5.1A UNIVERSAL PER-STEP CONTROL LOOP & COMPARE/REASON PRIMITIVES\n\nSTATUS\n  RATIFIED — ADDITIVE.\n  Patch class: Architecture / Execution / Orchestration / Epistemics.\n  Topology impact: none. The authoritative architecture remains an expandable typed graph.\n  Primary integration: Engine.Orchestrate/AAP, Engine.Reason, Engine.Compare, Capability.Knowledge,\n  Engine.Proof, Capability.State.EventService and Engine.Dependency.\n\nCORE PRINCIPLE\nEvery meaningful state transition in Garden is a controlled step: observed, typed, compared, reasoned, selected,\nguarded, executed/derived/communicated, observed again, compared again and recorded.\n\nThis does not mean every transition runs the full heavyweight Reason/Proof/Safety stack. Every meaningful step carries\nthe cheap universal kernel at a baseline tier and escalates only when required by consequence, irreversibility,\nauthority, novelty, uncertainty, sensitivity, dependency impact, disagreement or applicable hard policy.\n\nCANONICAL UNIVERSAL STEP\n\n  Current State\n       ↓\n  Observe\n       ↓\n  Understand / Type\n       ↓\n  Compare actual vs expected / desired\n       ↓\n  Reason\n       ↓\n  Generate candidate transitions\n       ↓\n  Select candidate\n       ↓\n  Guard selected candidate\n       ↓\n  Act / Derive / Communicate\n       ↓\n  Observe resulting state\n       ↓\n  Compare expected vs actual\n       ↓\n  Record / Update / Learn / Escalate\n       ↓\n  Next State\n\nA pre-selection constraint filter may run before Select to remove obviously inadmissible candidates. It does not\nreplace the final effect guard on the selected transition.\n\nCOMPARE AS A UNIVERSAL CONTROL FLOW\n\n  two typed states/claims/candidates + comparison context\n  → determine applicable comparison dimensions\n  → retrieve relevant evidence, constraints and dependency context\n  → compare each applicable dimension\n  → preserve uncertainty, conflicts, materiality and incomparability\n  → produce ComparisonResult\n  → feed Reason / Select / Explain / Research / Escalate as applicable.\n\nLevel 3 owns the COMPARE(...) signature, ComparisonResult field contract and ComparePolicy implementation details.\nTechnicalRef: [T-COMPARE-CONTRACT]\n\nREASON AS A TIERED UNIVERSAL PRIMITIVE\n\n  R0 — Transition Sanity\n       Does the transition follow at the declared abstraction? Is it obviously contradictory or inadmissible?\n       Intended cost: extremely cheap.\n\n  R1 — Local Reasoning\n       Choose among a small local candidate set; cheap.\n\n  R2 — Contextual Reasoning\n       Use knowledge, dependencies, constraints and multiple alternatives; moderate.\n\n  R3 — Deep Reasoning\n       Theory selection/composition, simulation, mathematical analysis, adversarial comparison and broader search;\n       expensive.\n\n  R4 — Critical Reasoning\n       Maximum bounded assurance appropriate to the scope, independent challenge and required governance/human\n       escalation; highest planned tier.\n\nReason is logically present at every meaningful step, but the active tier is selected by AAP. A tier is an assurance\ndepth, not a claim that real compute cost follows a universal fixed multiplier.\n\nVERITY / PROOF TIERS\n\n  V0 — structural/type/invariant sanity.\n  V1 — ordinary validation.\n  V2 — evidence/dependency/assumption checking.\n  V3 — independent/deeper verification.\n  V4 — formal/high-assurance verification appropriate to the critical scope.\n\nSAFETY TIERS\n\n  S0 — fast boundary/sanity guard.\n  S1 — local constraint check.\n  S2 — environment and consequence verification.\n  S3 — independent/deeper trajectory, human-presence or domain safety validation where applicable.\n  S4 — maximum bounded K0P/emergency-readiness/high-criticality assurance for the declared scope.\n\nA higher Reason tier does not automatically imply numerically identical Verity or Safety tiers. AAP derives each\nminimum floor independently from the operation and its obligations. Hard predicates may force a minimum tier regardless\nof any weighted risk score.\n\nTHREE ACTIVATION CLASSES\n\n  CLASS 1 — UNIVERSAL SEMANTIC / CONTROL KERNEL\n    GSL / Semantic Typing\n    Context\n    Time\n    Compare\n    Reason\n    Dependency\n    Verity / Proof, tiered\n    Safety, tiered\n    Constitution / Rights\n    AAP\n    Events / Provenance\n    Authority whenever an externally meaningful effect or authority-sensitive state change occurs\n\n  CLASS 2 — CONTEXT-TRIGGERED CROSS-CUTTING CAPABILITIES\n    Privacy\n    Policy\n    Law\n    Compliance\n    Access\n    Governance\n    Continuity\n    Emergency\n    Economy / Resource Allocation\n    Explanation\n\n  CLASS 3 — DOMAIN / EXECUTION CAPABILITIES\n    Mathematics\n    Research\n    Search\n    Software\n    Hardware\n    Network\n    Fabric\n    Energy\n    Mobility\n    Space\n    Health / Medicine\n    Industry\n    Education\n    Quantum\n    and any future typed domain/execution capability.\n\nPER-STEP TRANSITION ARTIFACT\n\nEvery meaningful transition emits a TransitionStep recording the before/after control-loop state and applicable\nassurance outcome. Level 2 owns the flow; Level 3 owns the field-level TransitionStep contract.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-PROCESS] [T-TRANSITION-STEP]\nSchemaRef: SCHEMA-B23C042B9C.\n\nREASON AND COMPARE OCCUR ON BOTH SIDES OF EFFECTFUL ACTION\n\n  BEFORE\n    current state\n      → compare actual/desired and candidate alternatives\n      → reason\n      → select\n      → guard.\n\n  AFTER\n    effect\n      → observe\n      → compare expected/actual\n      → reason about material discrepancy\n      → accept, correct, retry, replan, research, escalate, learn or terminate.\n\nA non-effectful pure derivation still has a typed comparison/reasoning/verification closure appropriate to its class;\nan external-world observation step additionally closes the expected-versus-observed loop.\n\nRECURSIVE CONTROL MODEL\n\n  S_t\n    --Observe--> O_t\n    --Compare+Reason--> D_t\n    --Select+Guard--> A_t\n    --Execute--> S_(t+1)\n    --Observe+Compare--> E_t\n\nwhere:\n  S_t       = current state;\n  O_t       = observation;\n  D_t       = decision/candidate selection;\n  A_t       = authorized transition/effect;\n  S_(t+1)   = resulting state;\n  E_t       = typed discrepancy between expected and observed result.\n\nA material E_t may route to:\n  CORRECT | RETRY | REPLAN | RESEARCH | ESCALATE | LEARN | TERMINATE.\n\nMEANINGFUL-TRANSITION BOUNDARY\nThe universal loop applies at declared semantic process boundaries. It does not require Garden to materialize a\nTransitionStep for every processor instruction, tensor operation or neural activation. A lower-level implementation\noperation inherits the enclosing transition contract unless it crosses a declared effect, state, authority, epistemic,\nresource, persistence, audit or risk boundary that requires its own TransitionStep.\n\nCOMPARE POLICY — FOUNDATIONAL HUMAN CONTRACT\nComparePolicy controls how one comparison is interpreted. It declares:\n  comparison dimensions and metric family;\n  domain/unit/frame bindings where applicable;\n  tolerance or equivalence/non-inferiority region;\n  uncertainty/dependence treatment;\n  materiality threshold;\n  hard bounds that cannot be compensated by other dimensions;\n  escalation/research behavior;\n  validity scope and DesignEpoch.\n\nNo universal physical or semantic percentage is implied. A generated machine ComparePolicy schema is derived from this\ncontract.\n\nASSURANCE CONTEXT REUSE\nValidated predicates may be inherited through a scoped AssuranceContextToken carrying:\n  validated predicates and exact scope;\n  expiry/review condition;\n  DesignEpoch;\n  KnowledgeSnapshot;\n  EnvironmentCommitment;\n  dependency/assumption closure;\n  invalidators;\n  authority/assurance floor;\n  provenance.\n\nAssuranceContextToken is a reusable proof/assurance context, not authority to skip newly applicable obligations. A child\nstep verifies the token and recomputes all predicates invalidated by state, dependency, environment, authority, policy\nor\nDesignEpoch change.\n\nR0/V0/S0 may validate the token and recompute only what changed. Cache/proof inheritance never survives a relevant\ninvalidator. This allows universal control without re-running deep checks at every child step.\n\nQUANTITATIVE DISCIPLINE\nAAP may use continuous or categorical risk/assurance parameters, but bootstrap coefficients and thresholds are policy\nconfiguration, not constitutional truth. Important quantitative claims are typed as:\n  FORMAL_BOUND | MEASURED_VALUE | ENGINEERING_TARGET | MODEL_PROJECTION.\n\nPerformance percentages are not promoted to MEASURED_VALUE until an implementation and workload-specific benchmark\nsupport them.\n\nPROCESS-CONTROL INVARIANT FAMILY\nCanonical definitions: Annexure A.\nInvariant references: PROC-001..021.\n\nCLOSING RULE\nEvery meaningful step is a controlled process node.\n\n  Observe → Compare → Reason → Select → Guard → Execute/Derive/Communicate\n          → Observe → Compare → Update.\n\nNo conforming material step is invisible. No conforming persistent mutation bypasses the process graph. The baseline\nkernel is cheap; escalation is proportional; the typed graph remains the source of truth.\n\n5.1AA FUNCTION / RESULT / PARAMETER CONTRACT FLOW\nSemanticAnchor: [S-FUNCTION-CONTRACT]\n\nEvery executable function projects to a FunctionContract:\n\n  semantic inputs\n    -> typed preconditions\n    -> AuthorityRequirement\n    -> effect vector\n    -> typed ResultAlgebra\n    -> resource / termination bounds\n    -> dependencies\n    -> provenance / receipts.\n\nEpistemic, proof, safety, authority, compliance, migration and distributed operations cannot collapse\nUNKNOWN/STALE/BLOCKED/TIMEOUT/INCONCLUSIVE into an ordinary Bool/None result.\n\nOperational constants and thresholds use QualifiedProfileParameter and declare whether they are:\n  FORMAL_BOUND | MEASURED_VALUE | ENGINEERING_TARGET | PROFILE_PARAMETER.\n\nSystemRefs: [S-AAP] [S-PROOF] [S-SAFETY] [S-COMPILE]\nTechnicalRefs: [T-FUNCTION-CONTRACT] [T-CAPABILITIES]\nAnnexRefs: [A-REG] [A-INVARIANTS]\n\n5.1B PROCESS ARTIFACT, PARALLEL, RECONSTRUCTION AND DO_NOTHING CLOSURE\n\nCanonical artifacts:\n  ProcessPlan describes the typed graph before executable admission.\n  ExecutableProcessPlan is the admitted specialization with resolved versions, budgets, guards and receipts.\n  ProcessInstance is the live/version-bound execution state.\n  ProcessCapabilityContract is the architecture-object interface consumed by AAP.\n\nPARALLEL({P_i}) requires a ParallelCompositionContract declaring isolation, shared state, join condition, cancellation,\npartial-failure handling, compensation/rollback, timeout, ordering/finality and deadlock/livelock/fairness obligations.\nA failed child cannot be silently ignored merely because another child succeeded.\n\nRECONSTRUCT(x) emits a ReconstructionReceipt binding source records, signatures/attestations, membership epoch,\nenvironment, uncertainty and equivalence result. Reconstruction never recreates authority from untrusted history.\n\nDO_NOTHING is represented as a DoNothingProcess with baseline, expected cost/risk of inaction, validity interval,\nmonitoring and next-review condition. It is not an empty undocumented branch.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-4A2791B6A1 — ProcessPlan.; SCHEMA-D1885D2B35 — ProcessInstance.;\n                   SCHEMA-1A45DD9AFE — ProcessCapabilityContract.;\n                   SCHEMA-149C4E5DD6 — ExecutableProcessPlan.;\n                   SCHEMA-01A5340315 — ParallelCompositionContract.;\n                   SCHEMA-733AA0C3C4 — ReconstructionReceipt.; SCHEMA-19D7452766 — DoNothingProcess.\n\n5.2 POLICY ALGEBRA — PA v1.0a\nDecision domain includes:\n PERMIT\n DENY\n REQUIRE_APPROVAL(A)\n DEFER\n NOT_APPLICABLE\n INDETERMINATE\n CONFLICT\n SAFE_FALLBACK(Action)\n\nFormal structure:\n D = (DecisionSet, AND_D, OR_D, NOT_D, RESOLVE_D, precedence_D)\n\nPolicy composition operators include conjunction, disjunction, negation, precedence, exception, temporal/contextual\nactivation, obligation union, delegation and escalation. Policy content remains owned by its substantive authority;\nPolicy Algebra owns universal composition semantics.\n\nPrecedence / authority:\n Constitution/rights and K0P are non-bypassable but operate as independent hard dimensions.\n Law > valid delegated authority > operational policy > user preference > optimization within applicable scope.\n Human court/governance rulings do not bypass Policy; they become typed AuthoritativeRuling/PolicyArtifact inputs\n at the applicable legal authority.\n\nResolveStrategy registry:\n DENY_OVERRIDES\n PERMIT_OVERRIDES\n MOST_RESTRICTIVE\n ALL_REQUIRED\n FIRST_APPLICABLE\n ONLY_ONE_APPLICABLE\n HIGHER_AUTHORITY\n OBLIGATION_UNION\n ESCALATE_ON_CONFLICT\nArchitecture objects cannot invent new resolution semantics at runtime.\n\nPolicy evaluation produces a trace that preserves applicable policies, precedence/conflict handling,\nobligations, decision/effect and provenance. Level 3 owns the exact PolicyEvaluationTrace field contract.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-POLICY-TRACE]\n\nPA v1.0a formal caution retained:\n AND_D and OR_D are distinct operators and MUST NOT share one normative truth table unless equivalence is proven.\n When multiple simultaneously applicable approvals are required, approval obligations MUST compose as an explicit\n requirement set; no required approval may be silently dropped.\n SAFE_FALLBACK activation must be conditional on the declared failure/uncertainty path, not treated as an unconditional\n effect.\n\n5.3 DECISION ALGEBRA\nDecision Algebra is the typed effect/result sub-algebra used by Policy Algebra and other decision systems. It does not\nitself own policy authority.\n\n5.4 CONFORMANCE ALGEBRA\nPurpose: reusable closed semantics beneath compliance, audit and self-conformance.\n\nOperands:\n RequirementSet\n ApplicabilitySet\n ObligationSet\n EvidenceSet\n ProofSet\n CoverageSet\n StandardMapping\n ConformanceResult\n\nOperators:\n REQUIREMENT_UNION\n REQUIREMENT_INTERSECTION\n SCOPE_PROJECT\n MAP_STANDARD\n PROPAGATE_OBLIGATION\n BIND_EVIDENCE\n BIND_PROOF\n INVALIDATE_BY_DEPENDENCY\n COVERAGE\n GAP\n DIFF\n COMPOSE_CONFORMANCE\n\nConformance preserves pass/fail plus explicit inconclusive, unknown, not-applicable, blocked and stale outcomes.\nLevel 3 owns the exact ConformanceResult status lattice.\nTechnicalRef: [T-RESULT-ENUMS]\nAudit is not a separate Algebra at this stage.\nAudit = UPA process/profile + Conformance Algebra + Engine.Compliance + Engine.Proof + immutable AuditEvidence.\n\nInvariant references: Policy.POL-ALG-001..021..\n5.5 EVIDENCE COMPOSITION ALGEBRA\nPurpose: reusable dependence-aware composition semantics for empirical evidence without turning evidence into Proof.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-020D6F213A — EvidenceComposition..\nEvidenceDependenceModel may represent common-source, causal, methodological, statistical or conditional dependence; it\nis not restricted to a binary independent/dependent flag or a single universal correlation coefficient. Evidence\ndependency structures may contain cycles where the declared dependence model permits them.\n\nCore evidence flow:\n  evidence inputs + dependence/context model\n  → assess dependence\n  → corroborate / contradict / condition as applicable\n  → preserve uncertainty and source dependence\n  → produce a typed combined/conflict/conditional evidence state.\n\nLevel 3 owns the executable operation signatures.\nTechnicalRef: [T-EVIDENCE-ALGEBRA]\n\nCorrelated/shared-source evidence cannot be counted as independent merely because it appears in multiple records.\nDependence-aware composition uses the declared valid model; it does not apply an arbitrary universal down-weight.\nProbability/confidence outputs are permitted only when a calibrated probabilistic model justifies them. Engine.Proof may\nverify formal calculation/model-consistency obligations, but Capability.Knowledge, Engine.Research and Engine.Reason\nremain responsible for empirical credibility, source validity and applicability.\n\nInvariant references: EVID-ALG-001..010..\n5.5A ALGEBRA SIGNATURE INSTANCES\n\nThe Process, Policy, Decision, Conformance, Evidence and Bridge algebras are registered as concrete AlgebraSignature\ninstances, not merely described beside the generic schema. Their operand/operator sets, valid laws, non-laws, result\nalgebras,\ninteraction rules, proof obligations and versions resolve through REG-ALGEBRA-001. A law omitted from an instance is not\nassumed. In particular, parallel composition is not universally commutative at the effect level, AND_D and OR_D are not\ninterchangeable, and evidence corroboration is dependence-model sensitive.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-C6B28E5F7D — AlgebraSignature.; SCHEMA-D8E1601C54 — AlgebraInstanceRegistry.\n\n5.6 FUTURE ALGEBRAS\nExplanation, Authority, Dependency and Resource remain candidates only if later GSL-COMPARE demonstrates a genuine\nindependent closed composition semantics not already covered by Process/Policy/Decision/Conformance/Evidence,\nmathematics or typed graph machinery.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-AUTHORITY]\n\n12.1 RIGHTS / CONSTITUTION\nLevel 3 Detailed Technical Specification. Rights/Constitution are legitimacy constraints, not policy preferences.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-SAFETY]\n\nSafety-critical novelty, material sensor conflict or assurance collapse does not imply a universal halt. Existing\ncritical-continuity semantics remain authoritative: revoke unsafe autonomy, hand control to an independently qualified\nsafe controller, maintain the domain's minimum-safe continuity when safer than shutdown, and restore authority only\nafter required evidence.\n\n12.2 HARD SAFETY / K0P\nSemanticAnchor: [S-EMERGENCY]\n\nK0P constrains physical admissibility. It is independent of political/normative authority. K0P cannot create authority\nto perform an otherwise illegitimate act; Constitution cannot make a physically unsafe act safe. Execution requires all\napplicable gates.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-PROOF]\n\nQualified proof obligations AGI-SUB-P11..P13 cover degradation-ladder safety, hardware-attestation ceilings and\ncross-modal resolution consistency. The global bare P1..P24 namespace remains unchanged.\n\n12.3 PROOF\nProof checks formal structural obligations. ProofAttemptResult is multi-status as defined in Level 2 System & Flow\n  Design. ProofTrace is\nimmutable. ProofCertificate references a verified ProofTrace. Empirical evidence/test/attestation use separate receipt\ntypes.\n\n12.4 TRUST ROOT BOOTSTRAP\nRequired genesis/replacement model:\n independent external genesis root;\n reproducible-build ceremony;\n checker/kernel diversity;\n multi-party key custody;\n hardware measurement/attestation;\n independently reproduced binaries;\n constitutional replacement procedure for a defective frozen checker.\nA compromised checker cannot be the sole verifier of its replacement.\n\n12.5 PROOF OBLIGATION NORMALIZATION\n formal/non-formal assurance class;\n assumptions;\n logic/profile;\n applicability;\n criticality;\n dependency root;\n environment;\n timeout/resources;\n checker or validation method;\n status;\n accepted artifacts.\nBare P7 is an alias only if registry explicitly maps it to P7a/P7b; otherwise callers must use exact IDs. P10 bundles\nare split into exact sub-identifiers where necessary.\n\n12.6 CONTINUOUS SERVICES\nThe bounded-recursion termination obligation applies to recursive jobs. Long-running services instead use\nproductivity/liveness/non-Zeno/fairness, bounded-reaction and stoppability contracts.\nProof reference: P5.\n\n[S-PHYSICS-ASSURANCE] GSL-PHYSICS PROFILE & TEMPORAL TELEMETRY FLOW\nAssurancePlan / AssuranceReceipt is composed by AAP and can include:\n formal proof;\n test;\n simulation;\n empirical evidence validation;\n security assessment;\n privacy assessment;\n compliance;\n human/domain expert review;\n counterfactual replay;\n post-deployment monitoring.\nNo one basis silently substitutes for another.\n\nStatus: RATIFIED DESIGN INTENT / ADDITIVE / integrated in this typed hierarchy. GSL: v45.1 semantic baseline;\nrepresented via typed artifacts without claiming parser certification in this text. Topology: no new fixed core\narchitecture object required; maps to GardenCore.MathInterface, Domain.Mathematics/Physics and relevant\ncapabilities/domains.\n\nPURPOSE\nProvide one rigorous representation for physical quantities, units, measurements, reference frames, conservation,\nsimulation fidelity, model credibility, digital twins and multiphysics coupling.\n\nPhysical quantities carry typed units, dimensions, frames, uncertainty/precision, time and provenance as\napplicable. Level 3 owns the exact Quantity/measurement/model field contracts.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-36784E4C56 — ReferenceFrame.; SCHEMA-645CD80ECD — CalibrationRecord.; SCHEMA-D444C6E742 —\n                   Measurement<T>.; SCHEMA-82FC8A16D7 — PhysicalModel.; SCHEMA-CB933E0469 — SimulationRun.;\n                   SCHEMA-ECC58713E3 — ModelCredibility.; SCHEMA-A0D1C291B0 — DigitalTwin.; SCHEMA-43985C421E —\n                   MultiphysicsCoupling.; SCHEMA-751D4A0BA5 — PhysicalIngressEgressReceipt..\nGSL-PHYSICS v1.1 — TYPED PHYSICAL SUBSTRATE AND TEMPORAL TELEMETRY\n\nPHYSICAL INGRESS / EGRESS ACCOUNTING\nPhysical ingress/egress flow:\n  physical boundary crossing or transformation\n  → identify typed material/energy flows and measurement/calibration context\n  → conservation/uncertainty accounting\n  → disposition + PhysicalIngressEgressReceipt\n  → domain-specific validation/audit.\n\nLevel 3 owns the receipt field contract and conservation-accounting details.\nTechnicalRef: [T-PHYSICAL-INGRESS-EGRESS]\nInvariant references: PHYS-001..030, PHYS-IO-001..008.\n\nPHYSICAL TELEMETRY / DATA-ASSIMILATION FLOW\n  typed Measurement<T> and immutable source payload\n  → bind source event/observation time separately from ingest/processing time\n  → bind clock domain, synchronization uncertainty and sequence/causal metadata\n  → detect and classify latency, jitter, duplicates, gaps, late arrival and out-of-order delivery\n  → reorder/deduplicate only under the declared bounded watermark/window policy\n  → check units, frames, calibration, instrument/environment context and source provenance\n  → evaluate observability/identifiability for the requested latent physical state\n  → admit, reject, quarantine or defer the measurement through the current DataAssimilationProfile\n  → update the owner-qualified PhysicalTwin specialization in causal/event-time order where required\n  → propagate covariance/uncertainty, drift, staleness, model residuals and affected dependency invalidation\n  → continue event-loop monitoring or invoke the prevalidated fallback/controlled-safe-state contract.\n\nArrival order is never silently treated as physical causal order. A timestamp without a declared clock domain,\nsynchronization uncertainty and provenance is insufficient for certified assimilation. Non-identifiable or\nnon-observable state remains UNKNOWN/DEGRADED rather than receiving false precision. A failed assimilation loop cannot\nself-authorize a new fallback; its fallback must already be qualified for the exact physical scope.\n\nGSL-PHYSICS v1.1 retains the v1.0 quantity/unit/frame/calibration/model/simulation/conservation foundation and adds the\ntemporal telemetry, out-of-order/jitter, causal-order, identifiability and event-loop contracts above. Legacy\nratification labels PHY-027..030 resolve only through the version-scoped alias mapping to canonical PHYS-027..030;\nbare PHY-* identifiers are not accepted in a canonical Machine Package.\n\nRegistry reference: REG-PHYS-001.\nTest references: TEST-PHYS-001..010.\nDOMAIN INTEGRATION\nHardware, Mobility, Space, Energy, Industry, Carbon, Health, Quantum and future physical/biological domains use the\nshared physical substrate wherever physical quantities/models are involved.\n\nRECURSIVE PHYSICAL TYPING RULE\nEvery detailed schema in Mobility, Space, Energy, Health/Medicine, Industry, Carbon/Earth systems, Hardware, Quantum,\nrobotics and other physically instantiated domains inherits GSL-PHYSICS quantity/frame/measurement/calibration/model/\nsimulation/conservation/error semantics where applicable. Human-readable Vec<Float>, Float, raw coordinate, dose,\nenergy, velocity, radiation, temperature or similar untyped shorthand is non-normative unless the field is proven to be\na pure dimensionless implementation representation. Before machine admission it MUST be rebound to typed quantities,\nframes, uncertainty/error bounds, calibration/environment context and credibility/safety requirements as applicable.\nSimulation/prediction never becomes observation/evidence merely through this binding.\n\nVOLUME II — META-ENGINES, KNOWLEDGE, CAPABILITIES, DOMAINS, THEORY SYSTEM & CONTEXTS\n\nVOLUME EXPLANATION\nWHY:\n  Explain how Garden finds, reasons, compares, learns and applies domain/theory/context knowledge.\nWHAT:\n  Meta-engines, Knowledge, shared capabilities, Domain System, Theory System, Context System, AAP, GSL-COMPARE\n  and GSL-EXPLAIN.\nHOW TO READ:\n  Read this volume when asking \"how did Garden reach this result and what knowledge/model/context did it use?\".\n\n6.1 ENGINE.ORCHESTRATE — IMPLEMENTS AAP v1.0\nAAP is the Activation and Assurance Policy profile; Engine.Orchestrate is the engine that evaluates and executes it.\nThe engine controls which Garden machinery activates and how deeply an operation is assured. Current classification\nadditionally consumes RegimeRiskAssessment, IntegrityHealth,\nReasoningPreemptionContract and ProofResourceBudget where applicable. Before live IntegrityHealth exists, the immutable-\nroot-bound BootstrapIntegrityManifest and DiagnosticPrecedenceContract govern the narrow cold-start path.\n\nPER-STEP BINDING\nAAP is additionally the compiler/router for the Universal Per-Step Control Loop. For each meaningful transition it\nselects the minimum sufficient R/V/S tiers, trace depth, authority checks, resource budget and activated architecture\nclosure. The selected plan materializes TransitionStep records where the meaningful-transition boundary is crossed.\nAAP optimization may reduce depth/cost but cannot remove applicable hard gates or falsely mark an omitted check passed.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-REASON]\n\n6.2 ENGINE.SEARCH\nHybrid semantic + symbolic retrieval over typed Garden state; retrieval never implies truth/validation.\n\n6.3 ENGINE.COMPARE — GSL-COMPARE\nCompares external/internal proposals against canonical Garden using bidirectional gap analysis and multi-dimensional\nconstraint-aware comparison. Current comparison also models evidence dependence, regime instability/tail uncertainty,\nand comparator self-evolution assurance.\n\n6.4 ENGINE.REASON\nSelects/composes reasoning strategies. Reasoning != Proof != Evidence != Authorization. Current modes explicitly include\nABSTRACTION and backward preimage/reachability analysis for necessary-condition/root-cause/WHY_NOT reasoning. A typed\nClaimValidationPlan routes promotable/consequential results to all required formal, empirical, test, legal/authority and\nhuman-review paths.\n\n6.5 ENGINE.PROOF\nCanonical scope is formal entailment/model-checking obligations and structural proof. It does NOT manufacture empirical\nevidence, validate empirical truth, certify an entire deployment alone, or turn a test result into a theorem. Canonical\noperation name may remain Verify(...); canonical capability name is Proof. The canonical engine/capability name is\nProof.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-EXPLAIN]\n6.6 ENGINE.EXPLANATION\nProduces faithful audience-appropriate projections from actual typed traces. Explanation does not invent missing\nreasoning/evidence or raise epistemic/authority status. Redactions produce a RedactionReceipt where disclosure itself is\nlawful/safe, preserving contestability without leaking protected content. No separate Explanation Algebra is ratified at\nthis stage.\n\n6.7 ENGINE.POLICY\nExecutes Policy Algebra over actual PolicyArtifacts and context to produce PolicyDecision + PolicyEvaluationTrace.\nPolicy Algebra != Policy Engine.\n\n6.8 ENGINE.COMPLIANCE\nUses Conformance Algebra for source-bound normative mapping, safety cases, gaps, internal conformance assessment,\naudit, evidence preparation and authenticated recording of external certification. Garden assessment cannot create\noperational admissibility or official certification, and no status substitutes for proof/evidence actually supplied.\n\n6.9 ENGINE.DEPENDENCY\nOwns typed dependency graphs, impact closure, invalidation and SCC/fixed-point contracts. Cached results may bind to a\nCacheBindingManifest cryptographically committing the relevant transitive dependency/source/environment closure.\n\n6.10 ENGINE.TRANSITION\nCoordinates consequential state change; GTF is its high-assurance UPA profile.\n\n6.11 ENGINE.EVOLVE\nBranch/merge, historical replay, recursive improvement, counterfactual trajectory comparison and governed adoption. It\nalso runs the periodic Human Rights Reality Sweep, which may nominate candidate rights gaps but cannot create, remove or\nweaken constitutional rights without the applicable human constitutional process.\n\n6.12 ENGINE.RESEARCH\nEvidence acquisition, theory discovery, RTR, falsification requests and domain research. LLM-generated material is never\nautomatically Evidence. Epistemic diminishing-return policy bounds repeated low-yield investigation while Deep Archive\npreserves hypotheses, status, evidence history and reactivation paths.\n\n6.13 ENGINE.COMPILE\nCompiles GSL/CIR/target artifacts under translation validation, typed dependencies and independent verification.\nGenerated code is not trusted merely because the generator is trusted.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-KNOWLEDGE]\n\nInterpretationValidationReceipt binds the source-to-intent derivation. ExternalEffectLedger and\nReplicaEquivalenceAttestation are provenance/freshness-bound evidence records; their existence alone does not establish\ntruth, safety or authority.\n\n7.1 KNOWLEDGE & STATE\nCapability.Knowledge\n owns canonical claim/evidence/epistemic-state semantics.\nCapability.Knowledge.Curation Capability.State.TimeService Capability.State.EventService\n\nEvent flow binds payload semantics to source/attestation, time/causal order, DesignEpoch/knowledge/environment\ncontext, privacy/retention, delivery state, epistemic status and provenance. Level 3 owns the EventEnvelope field\ncontract.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-EVENT-CONTRACT]\n\nRaw bytes are serialized representation, not semantic type.\n\nPrivacy learned/derived-data correction:\nDerivedDataImpact = graph of source data → derivatives/models/caches/replicas.\nLearned/derived-data correction preserves distinct verified deletion, unlearning/retraining, quarantine,\nresidual-risk and unestablished-erasure outcomes. Level 3 owns the exact result lattice.\nTechnicalRef: [T-RESULT-ENUMS]\nGarden never promises literal erasure of learned influence unless established under the declared threat/model scope.\n\n7.2 COMPUTE & EXECUTION\nCapability.Compute.Runtime Capability.Compute.Execution Capability.Compute.NativeInterface Capability.Compute.Software\nExecution requires conjunction of legitimacy, physical safety, authenticity, authorization and policy gates.\n\n7.3 INFRASTRUCTURE\nCapability.Infrastructure.Hardware Capability.Infrastructure.Network Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric\nDistributed semantics explicitly distinguish:\n eventual CRDT convergence;\n quorum/membership state;\n high-impact canonical finality;\n partition/partial-delivery status;\n visibility point;\n conflict ownership;\n FLP/CAP assumptions.\n\"Converged\" is never asserted when unknowable under the declared partition model.\n\n7.4 TRUST & PROTECTION\nCapability.Trust.Safety and Capability.Trust.Security preserve explicit active, degraded, emergency, stale/fallback\nand halt states. K0P never uses an OVERRIDDEN state. Level 3 owns the exact SafetyEnvelope status lattice.\nTechnicalRef: [T-RESULT-ENUMS]\n\nSecurity uses a versioned CryptographicSuite that binds the active cryptographic components, parameters,\nkey lifecycle, entropy/attestation, side-channel/fault profile and migration/deprecation state. Level 3 owns its exact\nfield contract.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-CRYPTO-SUITE]\n\n\"PQC encryption\" is not used as an imprecise blanket label.\n\n7.5 AUTHORITY, LAW & GOVERNANCE\nCapability.Authority.Law Capability.Authority.Governance\n\nEXISTING LAW / GOVERNANCE ADJUDICATION WORKFLOW\n(NOT THE EXCLUDED FUTURE JUSTICE SYSTEM / CanonicalCrimeOntology PATCH):\n Complaint/Petition\n Investigation\n Prosecution/Civil/Administrative Claim\n Defense/Representation\n Evidence\n Independent Trial/Adjudication\n Judgment\n Remedy/Sentence\n Appeal\n Constitutional Review\n Enforcement\n Rehabilitation/Restoration where lawful/appropriate\n Systemic Justice Audit/Reform\n\nLegal artifacts include proceeding type, jurisdiction, authority, standard of proof, remedy, appeal, recusal,\ntemporality, ambiguity/conflict and provenance. Law predicates use typed applicability/ambiguity results, not Bool.\nLegalSourceAuthenticityProfile distinguishes official source identity/status from applicability.\nComparativeRightsSynthesisProfile preserves jurisdictional/minority protections and cannot create legal authority by\naggregation.\n\nGovernance thresholds are constitutional/jurisdictional parameters, not universal magic numbers. Fundamental rights\nremain hard floors.\n\n7.5A RIGHTS-SYNTHESIS, PARTICIPATION AND RIGHTS-GAP ESCALATION\nComparativeRightsSynthesisProfile follows:\n  research synthesis → rights/conflict/minority analysis → qualified human/legal review → governance proposal →\n  constitutional non-regression check → RATIFY | REMAIN_CONTEXTUAL | REJECT | UNRESOLVED.\nIt cannot directly create law or constitutional authority.\n\nGovernanceParticipationProfile binds its electorate/eligibility and decision class to Proposal, Vote, VoteResult,\nAssemblyRecord, dissent, appeal and review records. Participation statistics never substitute for rights or truth.\n\nHumanRightsRealitySweep emits RightsGapEscalation, which routes CandidateRight/rights-gap evidence to the competent\nconstitutional bodies with urgency, affected persons, interim non-regression controls, dissent and next-review state.\nEvolution may nominate; it cannot ratify the right by itself.\n\nSchema reference: SCHEMA-0FC8536C7B — RightsGapEscalation.\n\n7.6 RESILIENCE\nEmergency, Continuity, Offline. Emergency never becomes authorization-free. A pre-issued EmergencyContract substitutes a\nnarrow emergency authority path for ordinary deliberation. Thresholds, sensor counts, duration, quorum, renewals and\ntiming are domain/risk profile values unless truly universal by proof/constitution.\n\n7.7 PLATFORM\nExtension, Development, UI shell, model/tool registries and development tooling.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-DOMAINS]\n\nGenerated domain projection may omit routine boilerplate only when the retained SchemaID, profile, types, unique\nconstraints, authority/consent/safety/evidence semantics and generation inputs determine the projection without a new\ndesign decision. Generated prose never invents authority or domain safety rules.\n\nDOMAINPACKAGE META-SCHEMA\nSchema references: SCHEMA-EEB95E26DE — DomainPackage..\n8.1 DOMAIN.MATHEMATICS\nM0 Foundations\n logic, proof theory, foundations, computability.\nM1 Structure\n algebra, linear algebra, graph/order theory, topology, geometry, category/refinement/concurrency.\nM2 Continuous & Dynamics\n calculus, differential equations, dynamical systems, control mathematics, numerical dynamics.\nM3 Uncertainty & Information\n probability, statistics, stochastic processes, Bayesian methods, information.\nM4 Computation & Optimization\n algorithms, complexity, numerical methods, optimization, solver theory.\n\nMathematical TheoryArtifacts/AlgorithmArtifacts/SolverArtifacts live here or under a domain-specific package.\nGardenCore.Formal.MathInterface defines universal representation contracts only.\n\n8.2 PHYSICAL SCIENCES\nPhysics, Chemistry, Materials, Astronomy/Cosmology.\nGSL-PHYSICS provides shared quantity/frame/measurement/model/simulation semantics.\n\n8.3 EARTH SCIENCES\nGeology → mineralogy, petrology, structural geology, sedimentology, geochemistry, geophysics, paleontology. Raw dose:Float is prohibited as a semantic order.\n\nPURPOSE\nA Theory Library is not merely a list. Garden continuously catalogues, checks, compares, revalidates and discovers where\neach theory actually applies.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-A3C74B6120 — TheoryArtifact..\nTheory status remains explicit and separate from formal proof status and empirical validation status. Level 3 owns the\nexact TheoryStatus lattice.\nTechnicalRef: [T-RESULT-ENUMS]\nSchema references: SCHEMA-E79230B607 — TheoryApplicabilityResult..\nSemanticAnchor: [S-THEORY]\n\nTHEORY EQUATION CONTRACT\nSemanticAnchor: [S-THEORY-EQ]\n\nEvery material theory equation binds variable types, dimensions/units, domain or manifold/graph operators,\nreference frames, assumptions, boundary/initial conditions, stochastic/numerical semantics, validity/breakdown\n  conditions\nand falsifiers before it can support formal proof or operational use.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-THEORY-EQ]\nTheoryRef: [TH-THEORY-EQ]\nAnnexRef: [A-THEORY-EQ]\n\nTHEORY META-SYSTEM\n Theory Registry\n Theory Catalogue\n Theory Applicability Map\n Theory Dependency Graph\n Theory Evidence Graph\n Theory Comparison\n Theory Revalidation\n Theory Discovery\n Theory Historical Status\n Theory Explanation\n\nCLAIMED APPLICABILITY != VALIDATED APPLICABILITY.\nA theory author or prompt may claim universal scope; Garden independently tests assumptions, evidence, domain fit,\ncounterexamples and competing theories. Search/Reason/Research/GSL-COMPARE may propose applicability; empirical support\nis maintained by Knowledge/Research; formal obligations by Proof; AAP selects depth.\n\nCANONICAL PUBLIC THEORY NAMING RULE\nPublic human-facing names favor ordinary descriptive language. Stable acronyms and historical technical names remain\nin parentheses for lineage, code, schemas and prior-art tracing:\n  Layered Reality (Stratified Reality Theory / SRT)\n  Persistent State (Valence Self Model / VSM)\n  Reactive Regulation (Reactive Persistent Machine / RPM)\n  Proactive Regulation (Proactive Persistent Machine / PPM)\n  Transport Phenomena (Transport Field Theory / TFT)\n  Emergency Response (Functional Emergency Theory / FET)\n  Structural Coherence (Structural Coherence Theory / SCT)\nA display-name change never changes semantic identity, evidence status or theory applicability.\n\nComplete canonical theory definitions are owned by Garden_Theories_v14.8.5_2026-08-27.txt [TH-*].\nThis System file owns theory activation, applicability and theory-to-reality flow, not duplicate theory equations.\n\nCURRENT GARDEN CORE THEORY CATALOGUE\nSchema references: SCHEMA-18DF6B1AEF — TheoryDemarcationRecord.\n\n  Layered Reality (Stratified Reality Theory / SRT)\n    Role       : Cross-stratum structure and applicability.\n    Boundary   : Architectural/meta-model unless a specialization makes testable empirical claims.\n    Falsifier  : Failed specialized predictions narrow or reject that specialization; OUT_OF_MODEL/UNKNOWN is not\n                 confirmation.\n\n  Persistent State (Valence Self Model / VSM v2.0)\n    Role       : Typed, context-bound, multiscale persistent operational state; optional valence projection, lineage,\n                 observability and bounded coupling.\n    Boundary   : Engineering state model; does not establish subjective experience.\n    Falsifier  : Declared state-dynamic predictions must be tested; failure narrows or refutes the applicable model.\n\n  Reactive Regulation (Reactive Persistent Machine / RPM v2.0)\n    Role       : Typed reactive regulation over declared state spaces using auditable agents/operators, aggregation\n                 and validators.\n    Boundary   : Regulation proposal only; reference/consensus/stability do not create authority or Safety.\n    Falsifier  : Geometry, validator, stability, composition or controlled-behavior failure narrows/rejects the model.\n\n  Proactive Regulation (Proactive Persistent Machine / PPM v2.0)\n    Role       : Typed asymmetric/proactive regulation with auditable adaptive fidelity.\n    Boundary   : H(z) is internal preference, not hazard; fast paths preserve mandatory gates.\n    Falsifier  : Asymmetry, fidelity, transition, efficiency or FET-separation failure narrows/rejects the model.\n\n  Regulation flow:\n    VSM state/context -> RPM reactive proposal -> optional PPM asymmetry/adaptive fidelity -> existing\n    CouplingCompositionProfile -> Constitution/rights/K0P/HSA/Safety/Policy/authority -> execution.\n    RPM/PPM may request FET assessment; independent hazard + EmergencyAdmissionContract remains required.\n\n  Transport Phenomena (Transport Field Theory / TFT v1.1)\n    Role       : Typed domain-parametric evolution/transport model family over continuous, graph, probabilistic, agent\n                 or hybrid domains.\n    Boundary   : Effective mathematical framework only when state/domain/operators/closure/validity are declared;\n                 cross-domain analogy alone has no physical or causal authority.\n    Falsifier  : Domain predictions must beat declared baselines; analogy alone is never Evidence.\n\n  Emergency Response (Functional Emergency Theory / FET)\n    Role       : Bounded emergency-reflex architecture.\n    Boundary   : Engineering control/safety model.\n    Falsifier  : Trigger quality, false-positive/negative rate, timing and safe-state behavior are empirically testable.\n\n  Structural Coherence (Structural Coherence Theory / SCT)\n    Role       : Structural coherence and epistemic consistency.\n    Boundary   : Formal/engineering framework; empirical only where metrics predict outcomes.\n    Falsifier  : Coherence claims require formal or empirical validation; failed metrics/predictions cause narrowing or\n                 rejection.\n\nThese seven remain current Garden core theories. None is protected from refutation by invoking a\ntranscendent/out-of-model\nstratum. OUT_OF_MODEL means the theory did not model the case; it does not validate the theory. Each theory carries its\nactual formal, empirical and application status separately.\n\nVSM / TFT / MAC TRACEABILITY FLOW\n  PersistentStateModelProfile [VSM v2.0]\n    -> optional PersistentStateModelProfileRef in TransportPhenomenaModelProfile when that state is transported\n    -> TFT applies only when a declared transport domain/operator mapping is valid\n    -> multi-agent sharing/aggregation additionally enters MAC authority/privacy/lineage constraints.\nThis binding preserves state ontology and HSA/TED scope; it does not make TFT mandatory or transfer authority between\nagents.\nTechnicalRefs: [T-VSM] [T-TFT] [T-MAC]. TheoryRefs: [TH-VSM] [TH-TFT].\n\nOTHER THEORY CATALOGUES\n Mathematical theories\n Physics theories\n Chemistry theories\n Biological theories\n Geological theories\n Medical theories\n Economic/social theories\n Engineering/computing theories\n Domain-specific and cross-domain future theories\n\nTHEORY REVALIDATION\nNew evidence, changed assumptions, changed environment, dependency update or contradiction propagates\nREVALIDATION_REQUIRED. A previously accepted formal proof remains valid only under its declared assumptions; current\nreal-world applicability can become stale/refuted.\n\nContext is a first-class binding of scope, environment, jurisdiction, locale, authority and validity. GSL Core Object\nCONTEXT is universal semantic meaning; Context.LocaleCulture is a service/domain binding.\n\nContext\n├── World\n│ ├── Earth\n│ ├── Moon\n│ ├── Mars\n│ └── future declared environments\n├── Jurisdiction\n│ ├── Nation\n│ ├── State/Province\n│ └── City\n├── Institution\n├── Community\n├── Personal\n├── Research\n├── Locale/Culture\n└── Operating Environment\n ├── normal\n ├── emergency\n ├── offline\n ├── simulation\n └── experiment\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-CONTEXT]\n\nContext participates directly in AdaptationScope validity, hardware-capability validity and minimum-risk fallback\nselection.\n\nBRANCH RULE\nUniversal semantics remain shared; contextual branches specialize applicable law, culture, language, resources,\ninstitutions, environment and policy while maintaining explicit BranchBinding to parent and provenance.\n\nAAP v1.0 — ACTIVATION AND ASSURANCE POLICY\nStatus: RATIFIED / ADDITIVE / integrated into Engine.Orchestrate.\nPrimary principle:\nFor every requested operation, Garden determines which engines/capabilities/domains activate; which checks are mandatory\nor optional; required verification depth; resource/time limits; human approval requirements; and fallback on\nuncertainty/failure/timeout. Safety emergencies may substitute pre-authorized emergency paths for ordinary deliberation\nbut never bypass Constitution/rights, K0P, authenticity or legitimate authorization.\n\nClassification dimensions:\n CONSEQUENCE\n IRREVERSIBILITY\n AUTHORITY\n NOVELTY_UNCERTAINTY\n SENSITIVITY\nAdditional domain-specific dimensions may refine a profile without weakening its minimum assurance floor.\n\nProfiles:\n  BASIC\n    Use       : Routine, familiar, low-consequence and reversible work.\n    Minimum   : Cheap retrieval/type/policy checks; fresh validated cache reuse is permitted.\n\n  STANDARD\n    Use       : Ordinary consequential reasoning or action.\n    Minimum   : Dependency closure, applicable policy/invariant checks, evidence freshness and ordinary verification.\n\n  CRITICAL\n    Use       : High consequence, material irreversibility or sensitive safety/security/financial/health authority.\n    Minimum   : Independent verification, deeper evidence/proof checks, stronger simulation/test and human/authority\n                gates.\n\n  SOVEREIGN\n    Use       : Constitutional/system-root/highest-consequence or canonical architecture changes.\n    Minimum   : Maximum bounded assurance, whole affected closure, independent challenge, explicit governance/human\n                approval, rollback/compensation and post-change validation.\nOperation pipeline:\n Receive\n → classify risk/profile\n → discover typed relevant closure\n → identify mandatory gates\n → plan resources/time\n → run assurance\n → authority/consent\n → execute/commit or defer/block/fallback\n → observe/validate\n → receipt/explanation\n → learn/revalidate.\n\nAAP artifacts (six canonical classes):\n OperationProfile\n ActivationPlan\n AssurancePlan\n ResourceBudget\n ApprovalPlan\n AssuranceReceipt\n\nBOOTSTRAP ASSURANCE KERNEL\nLive IntegrityHealth is unavailable before the first diagnostic cycle. Cold start therefore begins from a narrowly\nscoped, immutable bootstrap path rather than assuming HEALTHY state or recursively invoking the full AAP stack.\n\n  OTP/qualified immutable root\n   → verify minimal bootstrap-policy and approved boot-manifest hashes\n   → obtain TPM/secure-device measured-boot attestation\n   → load BootstrapIntegrityManifest with initial status UNKNOWN or DEGRADED\n   → execute only DiagnosticPrecedenceContract allow-listed L0/L1 diagnostics and safe initialization\n   → calculate live IntegrityHealth\n   → enter the ordinary AAP cycle or DEGRADE / ISOLATE / SAFE_FALLBACK / HALT.\n\nThe OTP/root anchor verifies the bootstrap trust chain; an attestation-capable protected key signs/quotes the runtime\nmeasurement. No unmeasured boot-time action gains ordinary execution authority. Bootstrap diagnostic precedence is a\npre-authorized minimum policy, not a bypass of Constitution, rights, K0P, authenticity or device authorization.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-3C6D43FED1 — BootstrapIntegrityManifest.; SCHEMA-EB18CA55BF — DiagnosticPrecedenceContract.\nInvariant references: BOOT-INT-001..007.\n\nCanonical typed AAP interface:\nSchema references: SCHEMA-9282CB1CC0 — OperationProfile.; SCHEMA-3C2C3C6B48 — ActivationPlan.; SCHEMA-73964D8DCE —\n                   OmissionJustification.; SCHEMA-8E29FDD648 — AssurancePlan.; SCHEMA-78D7F25E4F — ResourceBudget.;\n                   SCHEMA-F0C47CBD77 — ApprovalPlan.; SCHEMA-F2B2D99D22 — AssuranceReceipt..\nAAP binding rules:\n - selected_profile MUST be at least the maximum applicable requested, risk-derived, domain, policy and constitutional\n   assurance floor; no downstream component may silently lower it.\n - Every omitted potentially relevant object has an explicit typed omission justification. SOVEREIGN/critical profiles\n   may require formal closure evidence/proof; routine profiles may use validated dependency/effect closure receipts.\n - Execution requiring admission binds an APPROVED, fresh AssuranceReceipt to the locally verifiable\n   ExecutionAdmissionToken. Post-commit audit never substitutes for pre-execution admission.\n - PREAUTHORIZED_FAST / EMERGENCY_LOCAL execution uses authority granted before the emergency and remains bounded by\n   Constitution/rights, K0P, authenticity, consent/delegation and the declared emergency contract.\n - Distributed consensus/quorum requirements are protocol-, authority- and profile-specific and MUST be explicit when\n   applicable; no universal node count or percentage is implied by AAP.\n\nInvariant references: ACT-001..014..\n\nPOLICY / DECISION ALGEBRA INTEGRATION\n\nPolicy content is owned by the appropriate HumanCore/Capability/Domain authority. Algebra.Policy defines composition.\nEngine.Policy evaluates actual policies. AAP invokes Policy/Proof/Dependency/etc. according to operation profile.\n\nCOMPARE IDENTIFIER NOTE\nShort labels CMP-001..015 are valid only inside the Compare namespace and resolve to owner-qualified canonical\nidentifiers. Canonical machine references are owner-qualified: Compare.CMP-001..015 and Compliance.CMP-001..005.\n\nGSL-COMPARE v1.0 — COMPARATIVE EPISTEMIC & DESIGN ANALYSIS ENGINE\nStatus: RATIFIED / ADDITIVE.\n\nThree stages:\n\n1. Hybrid semantic + symbolic retrieval.\n\n2. Recursive closure + bidirectional gap analysis.\n\n3. Multi-dimensional comparative judgment with Proof/evidence/constraint checks.\n\nComparison normalization first extracts the proposal's claims/actions/relations/policies, assumptions,\nambiguities, context and provenance into a typed prompt semantic model. Level 3 owns the exact field contract.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-COMPARE-CONTRACT]\n\nCandidate retrieval MUST include mandatory symbolic sets before optional ranking:\n\nCandidate retrieval MUST include mandatory symbolic sets before optional ranking:\n ExactSymbolic\n ∪ MandatoryDependencies\n ∪ ApplicablePolicies\n ∪ RelevantInvariants\n ∪ TopKSemantic\n ∪ HistoricalMatches.\nSimilarity ranking may prioritize optional candidates; it cannot prune mandatory safety/constitutional/dependency edges.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-471066184A — RelevanceCandidateSet..\nStage 2 labels:\n AGREEMENT\n CONTRADICTION\n OMISSION\n AMBIGUITY\n NOVELTY\nA semantic atom retains parent context, qualifiers, scope, negation, temporality, authority and dependencies.\n\nSchema references: SCHEMA-BB850A168C — ProposalGapReport.; SCHEMA-EFE5F74DAB — GardenGapReport..\nComparison dimensions may include:\n evidence quality;\n formal consistency;\n empirical validation;\n explanatory power;\n invariant satisfaction;\n safety;\n authority/constraint feasibility;\n scope/applicability;\n simplicity;\n coverage;\n predictive success;\n cost;\n reversibility;\n historical performance.\nEach dimension carries value/ordering, uncertainty, confidence and evidence basis.\n\nHARD-GATE RULE\nConstitution/rights, K0P, required authority, mandatory invariants and minimum evidence/assurance are feasibility\nconstraints. Pareto comparison occurs among feasible candidates. Cost/speed cannot compensate for a violated hard gate.\n\nEPISTEMIC / ADOPTION SEPARATION\n epistemic_result = which claim/model is better supported?\n design_quality_result = which architecture is better on applicable design dimensions?\n constraint_status = what hard constraints/authority apply?\n adoption_recommendation = what should Garden do?\nCanonical status itself does not establish truth. Strongest valid evidence appropriate to claim type dominates canonical\nstatus on factual support. Mathematical claims require appropriate proof; empirical claims require appropriate evidence;\nnormative/legal adoption also requires legitimate authority.\n\nComparison preserves dominance/incomparability/inconclusive/unknown outcomes and routes adoption, retention, merge,\nresearch or escalation separately from epistemic support. Level 3 owns the exact enums.\nTechnicalRef: [T-COMPARE-CONTRACT] [T-RESULT-ENUMS]\nComparison produces a judgment preserving agreements, contradictions, proposal/Garden gaps, dimension\nresults, hard-constraint status, recommendation, unresolved questions, explanation trace and provenance. Level 3 owns\nthe exact ComparisonJudgment field contract.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-COMPARE-CONTRACT]\n\nCounterfactual option:\n World A = current Garden\n World B = Garden + proposal\nReplay representative historical/synthetic operations and compare correctness, safety, latency, resources, failures,\nproof burden and explainability where applicable.\n\nInvariant references: Compare.CMP-001..015..\nGSL-EXPLAIN\nCanonical engine name: Engine.Explanation. Question types include WHAT, WHY, HOW, WHY_NOT,\nWHAT_IF, WHEN, WHERE, WHO, AUTHORITY, EVIDENCE, UNCERTAINTY,\nALTERNATIVES, COUNTERFACTUAL and CHANGE.\nExplanation reveals the best available trace; it cannot invent evidence, authority, a proof or a missing causal path. An\nExplanation Algebra is NOT ratified in this release; it remains a future candidate only if formal closure is useful.\n\nVOLUME III — PROFILES, IMPLEMENTATION, OPERATIONS, EVOLUTION, REGISTRIES & DETAILED SPECIFICATIONS\n\nVOLUME EXPLANATION\nWHY:\n  Specify concrete profiles, operational contracts, detailed artifacts, lifecycle behavior, evolution, deployment\n  and registries needed to realize the design.\nWHAT:\n  Reference implementations, detailed engine/capability/domain contracts, theory catalogue, safety substrates,\n  operational flows, evolution/research/resource systems and machine-facing registries.\nHOW TO READ:\n  Read this volume when asking \"how would this actually be implemented, verified, operated and changed?\".\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-PROFILES]\n\nPROFILE PRINCIPLE\nA Profile is an implementation/research/deployment specialization spanning typed Garden objects.
