Original history remains immutable and provenance-preserved.\n\n15.3 INDEPENDENT MULTI-AGENT REVIEW\nIndependent teams/models may challenge changes. Majority/consensus is evidence about agreement, not truth or proof. Any\ndissent is explained and repaired where possible. Critical adoption still obeys Proof/evidence/authority gates.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-RTR]\n\n15.4 RTR — RESEARCH-AND-THEORY RADAR\nContinuously scans lawful/public/open research sources, tracks provenance/license/IP boundaries, creates candidate\nclaims/theories/upgrades, and never treats discovery/retrieval as validation.\n\nSemanticAnchor: [S-RESOURCES]\n\n15.5 CONTINUOUS RESOURCE ALLOCATION\nResources are incrementally allocated by marginal benefit/risk/uncertainty while protecting critical lanes, fairness and\nliquidity/stability constraints. Forecast ≠ receivable ≠ liquid ≠ allocated ≠ committed ≠ spent ≠ recovered. AAP\nsupplies operation-level resource budgets; resource optimization cannot weaken hard gates.\n\n15.6 INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY / OPEN KNOWLEDGE\nGarden may learn from facts, abstract ideas, mathematical principles and lawful public research. Public accessibility\ndoes not equal public domain. Protected expression/code/data/figures/proprietary implementation cannot be copied without\napplicable permission/license. Garden-generated eligible IP follows the Common Fund policy subject to law and\ncontributor agreements.\n\n15.5A PRODUCTIVE FINANCE — SYSTEM FLOW\nSemanticAnchor: [S-FIN]\n\nProductive-finance control flow:\n  productive need / instrument proposal\n    -> classify Credit | Participation | Ownership | Insurance | RealExposureHedge\n    -> identify payer, beneficiary and ultimate loss bearer\n    -> verify authority, ownership, collateral, leverage and cash-flow capacity\n    -> explain one-page human terms\n    -> issue primary instrument\n    -> bounded secondary transfer/batch auction where permitted\n    -> payment / impairment / restructuring / default / recovery / redemption receipts\n    -> continuing solvency, exposure and duplicate-collateral monitoring.\n\nUnknown ownership, hidden loss paths, unbounded exposure or duplicate collateral blocks issuance/transfer.\nPrice/yield/rating are Claims/Signals, not proof of value or solvency.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-FIN]\nAnnexRefs: [A-FIN] [A-INVARIANTS]\n\n15.7 RECURSIVE-CONFORMANCE / REFERENCE-CLOSURE FLOW\nSemanticAnchor: [S-RCC]\n\nCurrent physical-cognitive closure adds five SchemaIDs, 25 invariants, 25 tests, two registries,\nthree FunctionContracts and seven amendments\nto existing FunctionContracts, AGI-SUB-P11..P13 and three new System-flow anchors. Abrupt physical shutdown is not the default. It is selected only when the domain safety case proves\nit safer and feasible. Determinism, replication, majority agreement or software isolation alone does not prove safety\nor independence.\n\nPHYSICAL / BIOLOGICAL DO_NOTHING BASELINE\n\n  evaluation context + current physical/biological state\n    → select endogenous/environmental dynamics model\n    → project baseline trajectory over explicit horizon\n    → include decay, wear, disease, recovery, hazards, human/environment action and exogenous forcing as applicable\n    → propagate model/measurement/parameter uncertainty\n    → bind monitoring, invalidation, review and emergency thresholds\n    → compare intervention candidates against the evolving baseline.\n\nDO_NOTHING means no new intervention by the evaluated decision maker. It does not zero derivatives or freeze the\nenvironment. A missing required trajectory, horizon, uncertainty or monitoring condition makes the baseline\nINCOMPLETE/UNKNOWN, not a zero-cost comparator.\n\nQUANTUM/CLASSICAL CORRESPONDENCE\n\n  semantic/model objective + quantum/classical candidate models\n    → bind model class and validity regime\n    → declare state preparation, measurement and open-system assumptions\n    → declare coarse-graining/decoherence and approximation map\n    → declare CPTP assumptions where applicable\n    → classify Markovian/non-Markovian treatment\n    → prove/estimate error bounds and preserved observables\n    → identify breakdown indicators and boundary\n    → select required higher-fidelity quantum model and handoff\n    → QuantumClassicalCorrespondenceReceipt.\n\nLindblad/GKSL is admitted only under its applicable assumptions. It is a Universal Algebra alongside Process, Policy, Decision, Conformance and Evidence. It does not\ncreate a new top-level category or engine.\n\nCORE FLOW\n\n  semantic object / semantic operation\n    → Process Algebra identifies the intended transformation\n    → Bridge determines whether a valid computational representation exists\n    → bind representation kind, dimensions/axes, basis/frame, metric, precision and validity domain\n    → characterize exactness, approximation or loss\n    → construct operator plan\n    → construct representation plan\n    → analyze recursion where the operation is recursive/iterative\n    → optionally select a proof-aware optimization profile through AAP\n    → construct compute plan\n    → Compile / Runtime select and execute an eligible hardware realization\n    → capture representation result and optimization receipt where applicable\n    → validate projection/correspondence on the result path\n    → interpret the result back into typed semantic form\n    → route through the ordinary epistemic ingress/validation path\n    → Evidence or Knowledge only through their existing validation/promotion rules.\n\nREPRESENTATION FLOW\n\n  Semantic State\n    → Computational Representation\n    → Hardware / Execution Realization.\n\nExamples:\n  Claim    → embedding/vector representation → GPU/NPU tensor storage.\n  Relation → sparse/graph operator           → accelerator/distributed kernel.\n  Process  → operator graph                  → compiled/accelerated execution.\n\nThe computational representation and hardware realization remain projections. Neither becomes the semantic source of\ntruth merely because it is faster or numerically convenient.\n\nCORRESPONDENCE FLOW\n\n  encode\n    → apply represented operator\n    → decode\n    → compare with the semantic operation.\n\nExactness is admitted only when the declared semantic properties commute through the represented path inside the\ndeclared validity domain. Approximate/lossy paths preserve explicit bounds and lost properties. Incompatible,\nout-of-domain, stale, resource-limited and unknown results remain explicit.\n\nRELATION-PROJECTION FLOW\n\n  canonical typed relation\n    → context/time/epistemic binding\n    → relation-operator projection\n    → candidate numerical/graph path\n    → semantic validation against the original relation/model/assumptions/evidence.\n\nMatrix reachability can accelerate candidate discovery. prevent/detect/intercept/escalate the failure and did not do so becomes a separate\nSafeguardFailureFinding. Hand editing a generated view does not alter the authoritative H/S/T/THA/A\nsource suite.\n\nDefault audience paths:\n  Implementer → purpose/authority → flow → technical contract → schema → test/proof → deployment.\n  Auditor → scope → hard rule → invariant → evidence/proof → exception/UNKNOWN → receipt/provenance.\n  Scientist → theory → assumptions → equation/model → applicability → evidence/falsifier → uncertainty → research gap.\n\nRich reader projections let the renderer control visual line wrapping and may render source-bound diagrams as vector\ngraphics. Plain-text canonical source retains review-oriented formatting independently of rich-layout choices.\n\nVisualization contract:\n  DOC-PRES-VIS-001..006 require source-bound diagrams, explicit abstraction/omission, stable semantic identifiers and\n  faithful uncertainty/authority/status rendering. The renderer is selected through REG-DIAGRAM-RENDERER; rendering\n  syntax or visual style never becomes a second normative model.\n\nDesignated complex-flow coverage:\n  A documentation/presentation profile may mark a flow DIAGRAM_REQUIRED. The current minimum designated set is:\n    1. critical-continuity agency revocation and independent safe-control handoff;\n    2. ENG-DES import → semantic map → assessment → improvement → validation;\n    3. AI-assisted development → independent review → CI → merge → post-merge validation → deployment separation.\n  Every designated flow has at least one current source-bound DiagramProjectionSpec. Missing coverage prevents a\n  claim of complete designated-flow visualization but does not invalidate the underlying canonical architecture.\n\nFirst-use resolution:\n  The first materially technical use of a recurring term resolves to its canonical definition, schema, registry or\n  owner anchor under DOC-PRES-LINK-001. A display alias may be shown, but ambiguity or unresolved identity is surfaced\n  rather than guessed.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-PRESENTATION]\nAnnexRefs: [A-DICT] [A-CATALOG] [A-INVARIANTS]; generated reader glossary: [A-GLOSSARY]\n\nBOUNDED CHAOS / FAULT-INJECTION FLOW\nSemanticAnchor: [S-RESILIENCE-FAULT]\n\n  authorized resilience objective + exact target/environment roots\n    → FaultInjectionManifest\n    → BlastRadiusContract and protected-scope checks\n    → independent observers, abort guards and rollback/recovery readiness\n    → inject only declared faults within the admitted window\n    → continuously compare observed effects with the blast-radius envelope\n    → abort/contain on breach, uncertainty, observer loss or authority expiry\n    → recover and verify durable state\n    → ResilienceValidationReceipt\n    → failure-learning, dependency invalidation and revalidation as applicable.\n\nProduction fault injection is not implied by a test request. A qualification action\nmay replace a bare alias automatically only when exactly one current canonical target is valid; otherwise the result\nremains AMBIGUOUS and no automatic semantic edit is performed.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-LSP]\nAnnexRefs: [A-CATALOG] [A-REG] [A-INVARIANTS] [A-PROOF]\n\nFORMAL DEPRECATION / SUNSET FLOW\nSemanticAnchor: [S-LIFECYCLE]\n\n  deprecation proposal + exact target/version\n    → DeprecationRecord with owner, reason, replacement and affected closure\n    → compatibility, migration, safety, rights, legal and retention review\n    → notice and observation window\n    → ACTIVE → DEPRECATED → SUNSET_PENDING → REMOVAL_ELIGIBLE\n    → assess_deprecation_removal\n    → remove only after mandatory users/dependencies migrate or receive an authorized exception\n    → REMOVED | RETAINED | BLOCKED | STALE with provenance.\n\nDeprecation is not deletion. Historical identity, provenance and required reconstruction/retention remain available,\nand an expired date cannot override a blocked safety, legal, rights or dependency obligation.\n\nTechnicalRef: [T-LIFECYCLE]\nAnnexRefs: [A-CATALOG] [A-REG] [A-INVARIANTS] [A-PROOF]\n\nENGINEERING INTEROPERABILITY FLOW\nSemanticAnchor: [S-ENG-INTEROP]\n\n  registered external format/version + qualified adapter profile\n    → authenticate specification/API roots and adapter version\n    → map native elements, relations, units, requirements, behaviors and geometry\n    → preserve unsupported constructs, ambiguity and loss\n    → validate bidirectional source traceability and declared round-trip properties\n    → EngineeringDesignSemanticMap + validation result\n    → admit, conditionally admit, quarantine or reject the mapping.\n\nREG-ENG-DES-002 contains explicit entries for OMG.SysML/2.x, OMG.SystemsModelingAPI/1.x and ISO19450.OPM plus typed\nfuture-entry placeholders. CDDT owns cross-domain discovery/adaptation.\nShared retrieval/evidence is deduplicated by reference; completion of one process never falsely closes the other.\n\n\n========================================================================================================================\n[S-OCF] OPERATIONAL CLOSURE FLOW\n========================================================================================================================\nEvery architecture node/profile/domain/theory integration/implementation campaign follows:\n\nsource intent + current DesignEpoch\n→ extract finite material obligations\n→ preserve scope, assumptions, effects, evidence, resources, authority, privacy, safety, dependencies\n→ classify each unresolved item as DERIVATION | DESIGN | DISCOVERY | BLOCKED\n→ DERIVATION: generate from pinned semantics + qualified checker\n→ DESIGN: generate alternatives + Compare + Proof/Research/tests + bounded selection\n→ DISCOVERY: create authorised observation/experiment/evidence/decision protocol\n→ BLOCKED: retain explicit frontier and reopen condition\n→ composition check\n→ exact candidate qualification\n→ separate deployment authorization\n→ observe results/effects/cost/drift\n→ dependency-aware revalidation\n→ write gap/fault/fix/counterexample/receipt lineage to GSL-KR\n→ recursively audit the repaired system and its new assumptions/interfaces.\n\nMandatory OCF coverage classes:\n1 semantic execution; 2 knowledge ingestion/epistemics; 3 cognitive-strategy selection; 4 world-model learning;\n5 skill/procedure acquisition; 6 planning/information gathering; 7 durable runtime; 8 multi-agent independence;\n9 compositional assurance; 10 physical/reality validation; 11 recursive evolution; 12 resource/economic accounting;\n13 authority/privacy/safety; 14 security/adversarial operation; 15 deployment/federation.\n\nCompletion of a child does not close its parent until composition obligations pass. UNKNOWN/MISSING/STALE/INCONCLUSIVE/BLOCKED are not PASS. An admitted closure receipt is always scoped.\n\n\n========================================================================================================================\n[S-MSC] MINIMUM STABLE CORE + REPLACEABLE LAYERS\n========================================================================================================================\nLayer 0 — Minimum Stable Core (highest change threshold)\n  GSL semantic identity; ten Core Objects; epistemic distinctions; HumanCore/HSA; provenance/dependency/DesignEpoch;\n  proof/evidence state separation; OCF/CPI closure rules; protected trust/authority/safety roots; bounded termination.\n\nLayer 1 — Universal Garden operating responsibilities (slowly versioned)\n  Knowledge/GSL-KR; Reason; Compare; Proof; Dependency; Compile; Research; Evolve; Orchestrate; Runtime; Audit; Compliance;\n  GESTALT/APAO/GNR/Reliability OS; TRAIN/GAIA when physical control applies.\n\nLayer 2 — Theories, cognitive strategies and assurance profiles (continuously replaceable)\n  TFT/VSM/RPM/PPM, causal/world models, planners, inference strategies, memory strategies, domain models, safety profiles.\n\nLayer 3 — Implementations/backends (rapidly replaceable)\n  language models, symbolic solvers, theorem provers, learned models, search algorithms, compilers, databases and hardware backends.\n\nLayer 4 — Deployments (context-specific)\n  device, robot, car, factory, laboratory, data-centre, organisation, community and other operating environments.\n\nA lower layer constrains the admissibility of an upper-layer implementation. An upper-layer success does not rewrite the lower-layer\nfoundation. Foundation changes remain possible only through the strongest existing protected DesignEpoch/OCF review path.\n\n========================================================================================================================\n[S-TML] THEORY & MODULE CONTINUOUS-UPGRADE FLOW\n========================================================================================================================\nUpgrade triggers include:\n- new observation/evidence or anomaly;\n- failed prediction/test or changed environment;\n- better mathematical formulation;\n- safer/faster/cheaper implementation;\n- new external research/design found by CDDT/Search/Research;\n- security/privacy/authority defect;\n- dependency/toolchain drift;\n- new capability that closes an OCF frontier.\n\nTrigger\n→ create TheoryModuleReview\n→ bind current version, scope, evidence, dependencies and benchmarks\n→ search/derive/design alternatives\n→ preserve independent external prior-art provenance\n→ rights/admission screen\n→ compare current, candidate and simple baseline\n→ proof/experiment/benchmark as applicable\n→ OCF closure check\n→ staged admission\n→ monitor\n→ mark predecessor CURRENT_FOR_OLD_SCOPE, SUPERSEDED, CHALLENGED or REFUTED as evidence supports\n→ write all lineage to GSL-KR.\n\nNo periodic sweep invents a change merely to create activity. Zero upgrades is a valid result.\n\n========================================================================================================================\n[S-V151-UPGRADES] INTEGRATED UPGRADE FLOW\n========================================================================================================================\nConsequential external action:\nAI/agent proposal\n→ GNR/Runtime capability lookup\n→ explicit principal/delegation check\n→ deterministic ActionGate (policy/Compliance/Diamond as applicable)\n→ safety/Titanium/ASSURANCE gate where physical consequence applies\n→ dispatch\n→ effect receipt/reconciliation\n→ audit/knowledge update.\nNo cognitive model can grant itself a missing capability by text, peer consensus or tool discovery.\n\nExecution privilege:\nR0 protected safety/authority/checker roots;\nR1 qualified trusted runtime/system services;\nR2 bounded admitted agents;\nR3 generated/experimental code;\nR4 untrusted external content/data.\nInformation may flow inward only through declared validation/sanitisation/translation contracts; authority does not flow inward merely with data.\n\nKnowledge evaluation:\nmemory/knowledge candidate\n→ retrieval/availability test\n→ task-outcome comparison\n→ stale-error/consistency/cost analysis\n→ retain/revise/archive/supersede.\nHigh retrieval accuracy with worse downstream decisions is a failure.\n\nWorld-model loop:\nobservation → predictive model → causal hypothesis (optional) → plan/intervention proposal → authorised experiment/action → observation.\nPrediction/simulation remains distinct from causal identification and empirical evidence.\n\nEmbodied loop:\npersistent entity/state hypotheses → perception → prediction → plan → action proposal → safety/authority gate → effect → sensed outcome → correction.\n\nRecursive improvement metrology:\nwork package → AI/human contribution attribution → candidate → independent evaluation → accepted/rejected result → regression/cost record → GSL-KR.\n\n========================================================================================================================\nv15.1 EXTERNAL-DESIGN LEARNING / RIGHTS BOUNDARY\n========================================================================================================================\nGarden may learn from publicly described architectures, algorithms, benchmarks and safety patterns through CDDT/Research/TML.\nUnder the current no-import posture, v15.1 imports no external code, weights, datasets, substantial prose, diagrams or service dependency.\nExternal ideas are treated as prior-art/provenance and independently restated as Garden requirements. Any future direct import requires a\nseparate rights/admission review and does not alter ownership of independently created Garden material or pre-existing Garden rights.\nNo patent validity, patentability or freedom-to-operate conclusion is created by this design document.\n\n\n========================================================================================================================\n[S-CMUR] CROSS-MODEL AUTOMATIC-UPGRADE ADMISSION FLOW\n========================================================================================================================\nTrigger: TML/OCF/CPI/Evolve produces an upgrade candidate.\n\n1. Review under the predecessor process. Canonical Garden v15.5 is unchanged.\n\n## Eight stages\n\n1. **Observe / Discover** — inspect repo state, incidents, failed CI, open repair obligations, design coverage and new public research. Research may emit `CANDIDATE_ALTERNATIVE`; it may not declare an alternative better.\n2. **Packetize** — build one coherent-complete `GardenReviewPacket/v1` for one immutable `CycleID`. Canonical packet bytes have one SHA-256 `packet_hash` used by every blind reviewer.\n3. **Blind Review** — all model/specialist reviewers see the same packet and no same-cycle peer findings. Same-base specialists add coverage, not independent-family count.\n4. **Cross-Examine** — starts only after required blind findings complete for the same CycleID/packet_hash. Evidence is checked, not voted on.\n5. **Build / Repair** — implement the smallest verified-value change. Non-semantic work may use LIGHT; semantic work uses DesignEpoch-bound deltas.\n6. **Verify** — independently prove the original failure is gone and no regression or semantic drift was introduced.\n7. **Successor / Promotion** — compose admitted deltas into a complete successor. Immutable predecessor remains untouched. Protected canonical promotion remains human-only.\n8. **Pipeline Health / Hygiene** — audit lane health, repair backlog, branch/PR state, stale evidence, budgets, model yield and process complexity.\n\n## Coherent-complete ReviewPacket\n\n`PacketCompletenessReceipt/v1` passes only when:\n- exact public/private repo commits in scope are bound, or inaccessibility is explicit;\n- canonical version, DesignEpoch/source root and ProcessVersion are bound;\n- the bounded target text, source hashes and anchors are included;\n- each repo has a defined base/head diff or mechanically proven `NO_CHANGE`;\n- changed files and mechanically derived changed symbols are included;\n- dependency/reference closure and owner definitions are mechanically derived;\n- affected schemas, FunctionContracts, invariants, tests, registries and implementation bindings are included;\n- exact-head CI/test/environment evidence is included;\n- active freezes and unresolved/reopened findings touching closure objects are linked;\n- every exclusion has a machine-checkable reason; and\n- the closure frontier shows that adding another object would leave the declared closure boundary.\n\nMissing required material, ambiguous closure, stale binding or unexplained exclusion => `PACKET_INCOMPLETE`.\n\n## State coherence\n\nClock schedules are watchdogs, not authority to advance. A cycle moves only through:\n\n`PACKET_BUILDING -> PACKET_READY -> BLIND_REVIEW -> BLIND_REVIEW_COMPLETE -> CROSS_EXAM -> BUILDER_READY -> INTEGRATED -> POST_FIX_VERIFIED`\n\nSlow cycles remain isolated. Adjacent CycleIDs may not mix evidence. If cycles accumulate, intake is throttled instead of dropping findings.\n\n## Evidence classes\n\nThe producer assigns `claimed_evidence_class` and attaches the evidence object. A deterministic validator checks whether the evidence can support that class. Once validated, the class is immutable. Reclassification requires `EvidenceClassChallenge` with same-or-higher-class evidence.\n\n- **E4** reproducible executable trace, bound to exact commit/DesignEpoch/environment/command/result.\n- **E3** formal proof or machine-checkable contradiction with current premise bindings.\n- **E2** authoritative exact source/code/schema evidence.\n- **E1** reasoned argument.\n- **E0** unsupported assertion.\n\nIntegrators may not silently relabel evidence through selective citation.\n\n## Exact-head merge protocol\n\nUse merge-queue / rebase-and-reverify semantics. If main moves after verification, the old exact-head evidence expires. Rebase/update onto current main, rebuild affected evidence, rerun required tests/checks, then merge. Never silently merge X-verified work onto Y. No force-push or gate bypass.\n\n## Repair obligations and aging\n\nEvery verified defect becomes a typed `RepairObligation` with severity, first-seen cycle, age, attempts, blocker, exact affected objects, reproduction evidence, expected repair and tests.\n\n- CRITICAL preempts ordinary upgrades.\n- HIGH gains priority with age.\n- MEDIUM also ages upward.\n- `REPAIR_BACKLOG_PRESSURE` throttles new discovery/feature intake when repair debt exceeds policy thresholds.\n\nOpening an issue, freezing work or getting model agreement is not a fix.\n\n## Reviewer allocation\n\nPrimary yield signals are external outcomes: seeded-defect detection, independently `VERIFIED_FIXED` results, reproducible counterexamples, incident/regression attribution and false-positive rate. Challenger/cross-exam acceptance is secondary and capped. Preserve a fixed exploration/diversity share.\n\n## Human availability\n\n- `HUMAN_AVAILABLE`: normal operation.\n- `HUMAN_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE`: protected promotion pauses; routine governed work continues; admitted-unpromoted semantic work is capped at one major-candidate batch (normally <=10 deltas unless already MAJOR). Beyond cap => `DEFERRED_PENDING_PROMOTION_CAPACITY`.\n- `HUMAN_PERMANENTLY_UNAVAILABLE`: canonical promotion remains frozen until a separately admitted succession/continuity mechanism exists. Keep the same cap; already-authorized safety/correctness implementation against the last valid DesignEpoch may continue; issue a 90-day continuity-status review.\n\nProtected authority is never silently transferred.\n\n## Budgets\n\nRoutine public OpenRouter review keeps the existing ~USD 1/day ceiling. Expensive escalation uses a separate `EscalationBudget` and defaults to zero automatic spend unless explicitly pre-authorized.\n\n## Process change control\n\nSemantic changes to review independence, packet completeness, evidence rules, state transitions, merge/freeze rules, budgets, human authority, admission/promotion, pipeline-health semantics, or Process Algebra binding require a ProcessVersion bump. ProcessVersion N+1 is reviewed under N and cannot self-authorize. Pure implementation fixes preserving admitted process semantics may use the non-semantic track.\n\n## Pipeline health\n\n`PipelineHealthReceipt/v1` records, per lane: last successful cycle, attempts/completions, latency, missed/stale cycles, backlog, failure reasons, unresolved repairs, independently verified closures, false closures, stale evidence, packet-incomplete rate and health state `HEALTHY|DEGRADED|REDUCED_OPERATION|FAILED`.\n\nNo lane certifies its own closure counts. Unhealthy lanes reduce operation or fail closed until repaired.\n\n## Simplification\n\nQuarterly `PipelineSimplificationReview/v1` looks for zero-yield lanes, duplicate gates, rules that never fire, record types with no consumers, obsolete paths and high-cost/low-value checks. Removal is a first-class improvement. Rare but load-bearing safety/privacy/authority controls are not removed merely because they rarely fire.\n\n## Garden Process Algebra binding\n\nThe upgrade pipeline is not a separate orchestration framework. It is a governed Garden Process Algebra instance bound to canonical `REG-ALGEBRA-001`, `[S-PROCESS]#5.1`, and `[S-PROCESS]#5.1B`.\n\nEvery update action must emit or reference a validated `GardenEvolutionAlgebraUsageReceipt/v1` and an `AlgebraBoundUpdateReceipt/v1`. These receipts describe operator use; they grant no authority and do not prove Garden certification.\n\nThe Process operators are used as follows:\n\n- `SEQUENCE(A,B)` — ordered state transitions and gates.\n- `PARALLEL({P_i})` — blind peer reviewers operating on one identical `packet_hash`.\n- `COMPOSE(A,B)` — composition of validated stages without assuming extra algebra laws.\n- `BRANCH(c,A,B)` — semantic-vs-LIGHT routing, pass-vs-repair routing, and target-kind routing.\n- `RETRY(P,bound)` — bounded repair attempts.\n- `ROLLBACK(P)` — reverse a failed governed integration or promoted successor when rollback policy permits.\n- `COMPENSATE(P)` — remediate external side effects that cannot be erased by rollback.\n- `RECONSTRUCT(x)` — rebuild process state only from bound evidence/receipts; reconstruction never recreates authority from untrusted history.\n- `ITERATE(P,k)` — bounded continuous upgrade cycles.\n- `RECURSE(P)` — nested dependency repair only under explicit termination/resource bounds.\n- `TERMINATE(reason)` — stop on invalid authority, unresolved critical conflict, failed completeness, or exhausted safe retry bounds.\n- `DO_NOTHING` — required valid result when no change has positive verified value.\n\nNo associativity, commutativity, idempotence, distributivity or reordering law is inferred unless canonically registered. In particular, parallel reviewer effects are **not** assumed commutative.\n\n### One update process, three targets\n\nAll updates use the same algebraic control flow:\n\n`Observe/Discover -> Packetize -> PARALLEL(blind review) -> Cross-Examine -> BRANCH(semantic, full semantic path, LIGHT path) -> Build/Repair -> Verify -> BRANCH(pass, target-specific integration, RETRY/ROLLBACK/TERMINATE) -> Post-Fix Verify -> ITERATE or DO_NOTHING`\n\nThe target-specific terminal rule is the only major difference:\n\n1. `PUBLIC_REPO` — exact-head governed merge into the public repository.\n2. `PRIVATE_IMPLEMENTATION` — exact-head governed merge into the private implementation repository.\n3. `CANONICAL_DESIGN` — never edit the immutable predecessor; compose a complete successor candidate, then require separately protected human canonical promotion.\n\nPolicy Algebra applies only where a registered authority/approval operator is actually executed. Ordinary ActionGate checks must not be silently relabeled as Policy Algebra.\n\nConformance Algebra is required for requirement/reference/test/obligation closure and exact-head conformance. Decision and Evidence Algebra remain explicit `FRONTIER` where their executable operator signatures are not yet bound. Bridge Algebra is conditional and applies only when a registered semantic-to-representation transformation is actually invoked.\n\nTherefore Process v2 is **algebra-bound by construction**, but it must continue to report `semantic_compliance_proved=false` until every required frontier for the action is closed and a valid algebra validation receipt exists. `FRONTIER` never counts as compliance.\n","encoding":"UTF8","git_blob_sha1":"1be31e978cc50b87263e3382b898df145b0507ee","object_type":"RepositoryFileEvidence/v1","path":"CONTINUOUS_UPGRADE_PROCESS.md","repo_revision":"347b48f8b870e10dc1e8c956f1f116e90e628dbe","role":"PUBLIC_TARGET_DECLARED_REFERENCE_CLOSURE","sha256":"7ca67012532de885da7683eb89cc957a4fbe47b55d68c2463026683604e2db0a"},{"byte_length":1518,"content":"{\n This is a **protected process change**, not a canonical Garden v15.5 semantic edit. Implement as the smallest coherent ProcessVersion successor and review/admit it under the current predecessor process. ProcessVersion N+1 must not self-authorize.\n\n## Required ProcessVersion changes\n\n### 1. Coherent-complete ReviewPacket\nImplement `GardenReviewPacket/v1` + `PacketCompletenessReceipt/v1` with one canonical serialization and SHA-256 `packet_hash` consumed identically by all blind reviewers for a CycleID.\n\nRequired bindings:\n- exact public/private repo commits in scope (or explicit inaccessible status)\n- canonical Garden version, DesignEpoch/source root, ProcessVersion\n- bounded target/source hashes/anchors\n- defined base/head diff or mechanically proven NO_CHANGE\n- changed files and mechanically derived changed symbols\n- mechanically derived dependency/reference closure + owner definitions\n- affected schemas, FunctionContracts, invariants, tests, registries and implementation bindings\n- exact-head CI/test/environment receipts\n- active freezes and linked unresolved/reopened findings/RepairObligations touching closure objects\n- explicit exclusion manifest with machine-checkable reasons\n- closure frontier\n\n`COHERENT_COMPLETE` passes only when every mechanically declared closure object is included or explicitly excluded for valid reason and no further object can be added without leaving the declared closure boundary. Any ambiguity/unexplained exclusion/stale binding => PACKET_INCOMPLETE.\n\n### 2. State/event coherence\nClock schedules are watchdog/start opportunities only. Add immutable `CycleID` and enforce state transitions:\n`PACKET_BUILDING -> PACKET_READY -> BLIND_REVIEW -> BLIND_REVIEW_COMPLETE -> CROSS_EXAM -> BUILDER_READY -> INTEGRATED -> POST_FIX_VERIFIED`.\nNo adjacent-cycle evidence mixing. Slow cycles remain isolated. If backlog grows, throttle intake instead of dropping findings.\n\n### 3. Evidence classes\nProducer assigns `claimed_evidence_class` E0-E4 at finding time and attaches the evidence object. Deterministic validator confirms the class is supportable. Once validated, class is immutable. Reclassification requires typed `EvidenceClassChallenge` carrying same-or-higher-class evidence; integrators may not silently relabel/selectively cite.\n- E4 executable trace requires exact commit/DesignEpoch/environment/command/result binding.\n- E3 formal proof/machine contradiction requires current premise bindings.\n- E2 authoritative exact source/code/schema evidence.\n- E1 reasoned argument.\n- E0 unsupported assertion.\n\n### 4. Blind specialist boundary\nAll pre-cross-exam model/specialist reviewers consume the same packet_hash and remain blind to same-cycle peers until their independent finding is persisted. Same-base specialists add coverage but not another independent-family count. Cross-exam starts only after required blind findings complete.\n\n### 5. Research Scout classification\nReplace `BETTER_ALTERNATIVE` with `CANDIDATE_ALTERNATIVE`. Scout may state the dimension that may improve and attach raw evidence. Cross-exam decides whether it is actually better.\n\n### 6. Reviewer allocation\nPrimary yield signals: seeded-defect detection, independently `VERIFIED_FIXED` post-merge outcomes, reproducible counterexamples, incident/regression attribution and false-positive rate. Challenger/cross-exam acceptance is secondary and capped. Preserve a fixed exploration/diversity share to prevent feedback narrowing.\n\n### 7. Exact-head merge protocol\nUse merge-queue/rebase-and-reverify semantics. If main/base moves after verification, exact-head evidence expires; rebase/update to new main, rebuild affected evidence/packet, rerun required tests/checks, then merge. Never silently merge X-verified work onto Y. Use expected-head SHA and branch protections; no force-push/bypass.\n\n### 8. Persistent repair aging\nDefine typed `RepairObligation` fields: severity, first_seen_cycle, age_cycles, last_attempt_cycle, attempt_count, blocker, affected SHAs/paths/symbols, reproduction evidence, expected repair and tests. CRITICAL preempts ordinary work. HIGH gains priority with age. Add `REPAIR_BACKLOG_PRESSURE` when debt crosses policy threshold; throttle new discovery/feature intake until debt falls.\n\n### 9. Pipeline health\nImplement `PipelineHealthReceipt/v1` per active lane with last successful CycleID/time, attempts/completions 24h, late/missed/stale cycles, median/p95 latency where available, backlog, failure reasons, unresolved obligations, independently verified closures, false closures/stale evidence, packet-incomplete rate and state `HEALTHY|DEGRADED|REDUCED_OPERATION|FAILED`.\nNo lane may certify its own closure counts. Unhealthy lanes reduce operation/fail closed until repaired.\n\n### 10. Human availability\nDefine:\n- `HUMAN_AVAILABLE`: normal.\n- `HUMAN_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE`: protected promotion paused; routine governed work continues; admitted/unpromoted semantic work capped at one major-candidate batch (normally <=10 deltas unless already MAJOR); beyond cap => `DEFERRED_PENDING_PROMOTION_CAPACITY`.\n- `HUMAN_PERMANENTLY_UNAVAILABLE`: canonical promotion frozen until separately admitted succession/continuity mechanism exists; same cap; already-authorized safety/correctness implementation against last valid DesignEpoch may continue; 90-day continuity-status review.\nNever silently transfer protected authority.\n\n### 11. Budget separation\nRoutine public OpenRouter ceiling remains ~USD 1/day. Expensive escalation uses a separate `EscalationBudget` and defaults to **zero automatic spend** unless explicitly pre-authorized. Do not use routine budget merely to consume it.\n\n### 12. Protected pipeline change control\nSemantic changes to review independence, packet completeness, evidence rules, state transitions, merge/freeze rules, budgets, human authority, admission/promotion or pipeline-health semantics require ProcessVersion bump. N+1 reviewed under N. Pure implementation fixes preserving admitted semantics may use LIGHT/non-semantic track.\n\n### 13. Simplification\nAdd quarterly `PipelineSimplificationReview/v1`: identify zero-yield lanes, duplicate gates, rules never fired, record types with no consumers, obsolete paths, and high-cost/low-value checks. Propose removals/merges with dependency/risk/rollback evidence. Rare but load-bearing safety/authority/privacy controls are not removed merely for low firing frequency.\n\n## Architectural simplification\nPresent the continuous system as eight major stages rather than dozens of equal-level processes:\n1. Observe / Discover\n2. Packetize\n3. Blind Review\n4. Cross-Examine\n5. Build / Repair\n6. Verify\n7. Successor / Promotion\n8. Pipeline Health / Hygiene\n\nResearch, specialists, model families and test agents are workers inside these stages.\n\n## Required implementation/testing\n- machine-readable process policy + ProcessVersion artifact\n- deterministic packet/evidence validators\n- regression tests for missing packet fields, wrong packet hash/CycleID, unexplained exclusions, evidence overclassification, wrong-cycle cross-exam, stale exact-head merge evidence, repair aging/backlog pressure and health-state transitions\n- update public hourly workflows so packet finalization precedes all blind reviews and authoritative cross-exam occurs only after blind completion\n- specialist sweep must use same target/packet, not a different-offset target, for independent-family claims\n- preserve canonical Garden v15.5 unchanged\n\n## Current operational configuration\nChatGPT automations have already been updated provisionally to these fail-closed semantics, including a pre-review ReviewPacket Builder, state-gated reviewer/cross-exam/builder, Pipeline Health lane and quarterly Simplification review. (candidate)\n+\n+Status: protected process candidate. Review under the predecessor process. Canonical Garden v15.5 is unchanged.\n+\n+## Eight stages\n+\n+1. **Observe / Discover** — inspect repo state, incidents, failed CI, open repair obligations, design coverage and new public research. Research may emit `CANDIDATE_ALTERNATIVE`; it may not declare an alternative better.\n+2. **Packetize** — build one coherent-complete `GardenReviewPacket/v1` for one immutable `CycleID`. Canonical packet bytes have one SHA-256 `packet_hash` used by every blind reviewer.\n+3. **Blind Review** — all model/specialist reviewers see the same packet and no same-cycle peer findings. Same-base specialists add coverage, not independent-family count.\n+4. **Cross-Examine** — starts only after required blind findings complete for the same CycleID/packet_hash. Evidence is checked, not voted on.\n+5. **Build / Repair** — implement the smallest verified-value change. Non-semantic work may use LIGHT; semantic work uses DesignEpoch-bound deltas.\n+6. **Verify** — independently prove the original failure is gone and no regression or semantic drift was introduced.\n+7. **Successor / Promotion** — compose admitted deltas into a complete successor. Immutable predecessor remains untouched. Protected canonical promotion remains human-only.\n+8. **Pipeline Health / Hygiene** — audit lane health, repair backlog, branch/PR state, stale evidence, budgets, model yield and process complexity.\n+\n+## Coherent-complete ReviewPacket\n+\n+`PacketCompletenessReceipt/v1` passes only when:\n+- exact public/private repo commits in scope are bound, or inaccessibility is explicit;\n+- canonical version, DesignEpoch/source root and ProcessVersion are bound;\n+- the bounded target text, source hashes and anchors are included;\n+- each repo has a defined base/head diff or mechanically proven `NO_CHANGE`;\n+- changed files and mechanically derived changed symbols are included;\n+- dependency/reference closure and owner definitions are mechanically derived;\n+- affected schemas, FunctionContracts, invariants, tests, registries and implementation bindings are included;\n+- exact-head CI/test/environment evidence is included;\n+- active freezes and unresolved/reopened findings touching closure objects are linked;\n+- every exclusion has a machine-checkable reason; and\n+- the closure frontier shows that adding another object would leave the declared closure boundary.\n+\n+Missing required material, ambiguous closure, stale binding or unexplained exclusion => `PACKET_INCOMPLETE`.\n+\n+## State coherence\n+\n+Clock schedules are watchdogs, not authority to advance. A cycle moves only through:\n+\n+`PACKET_BUILDING -> PACKET_READY -> BLIND_REVIEW -> BLIND_REVIEW_COMPLETE -> CROSS_EXAM -> BUILDER_READY -> INTEGRATED -> POST_FIX_VERIFIED`\n+\n+Slow cycles remain isolated. Adjacent CycleIDs may not mix evidence. If cycles accumulate, intake is throttled instead of dropping findings.\n+\n+## Evidence classes\n+\n+The producer assigns `claimed_evidence_class` and attaches the evidence object. A deterministic validator checks whether the evidence can support that class. Once validated, the class is immutable. Reclassification requires `EvidenceClassChallenge` with same-or-higher-class evidence.\n+\n+- **E4** reproducible executable trace, bound to exact commit/DesignEpoch/environment/command/result.\n+- **E3** formal proof or machine-checkable contradiction with current premise bindings.\n+- **E2** authoritative exact source/code/schema evidence.\n+- **E1** reasoned argument.\n+- **E0** unsupported assertion.\n+\n+Integrators may not silently relabel evidence through selective citation.\n+\n+## Exact-head merge protocol\n+\n+Use merge-queue / rebase-and-reverify semantics. If main moves after verification, the old exact-head evidence expires. Rebase/update onto current main, rebuild affected evidence, rerun required tests/checks, then merge. Never silently merge X-verified work onto Y. No force-push or gate bypass.\n+\n+## Repair obligations and aging\n+\n+Every verified defect becomes a typed `RepairObligation` with severity, first-seen cycle, age, attempts, blocker, exact affected objects, reproduction evidence, expected repair and tests.\n+\n+- CRITICAL preempts ordinary upgrades.\n+- HIGH gains priority with age.\n+- MEDIUM also ages upward.\n+- `REPAIR_BACKLOG_PRESSURE` throttles new discovery/feature intake when repair debt exceeds policy thresholds.\n+\n+Opening an issue, freezing work or getting model agreement is not a fix.\n+\n+## Reviewer allocation\n+\n+Primary yield signals are external outcomes: seeded-defect detection, independently `VERIFIED_FIXED` results, reproducible counterexamples, incident/regression attribution and false-positive rate. Challenger/cross-exam acceptance is secondary and capped. Preserve a fixed exploration/diversity share.\n+\n+## Human availability\n+\n+- `HUMAN_AVAILABLE`: normal operation.\n+- `HUMAN_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE`: protected promotion pauses; routine governed work continues; admitted-unpromoted semantic work is capped at one major-candidate batch (normally <=10 deltas unless already MAJOR). Beyond cap => `DEFERRED_PENDING_PROMOTION_CAPACITY`.\n+- `HUMAN_PERMANENTLY_UNAVAILABLE`: canonical promotion remains frozen until a separately admitted succession/continuity mechanism exists. Keep the same cap; already-authorized safety/correctness implementation against the last valid DesignEpoch may continue; issue a 90-day continuity-status review.\n+\n+Protected authority is never silently transferred.\n+\n+## Budgets\n+\n+Routine public OpenRouter review keeps the existing ~USD 1/day ceiling. Expensive escalation uses a separate `EscalationBudget` and defaults to zero automatic spend unless explicitly pre-authorized.\n+\n+## Process change control\n+\n+Semantic changes to review independence, packet completeness, evidence rules, state transitions, merge/freeze rules, budgets, human authority, admission/promotion, pipeline-health semantics, or Process Algebra binding require a ProcessVersion bump. ProcessVersion N+1 is reviewed under N and cannot self-authorize. Pure implementation fixes preserving admitted process semantics may use the non-semantic track.\n+\n+## Pipeline health\n+\n+`PipelineHealthReceipt/v1` records, per lane: last successful cycle, attempts/completions, latency, missed/stale cycles, backlog, failure reasons, unresolved repairs, independently verified closures, false closures, stale evidence, packet-incomplete rate and health state `HEALTHY|DEGRADED|REDUCED_OPERATION|FAILED`.\n+\n+No lane certifies its own closure counts. Unhealthy lanes reduce operation or fail closed until repaired.\n+\n+## Simplification\n+\n+Quarterly `PipelineSimplificationReview/v1` looks for zero-yield lanes, duplicate gates, rules that never fire, record types with no consumers, obsolete paths and high-cost/low-value checks. Removal is a first-class improvement. Rare but load-bearing safety/privacy/authority controls are not removed merely because they rarely fire.\n+\n+## Garden Process Algebra binding\n+\n+The upgrade pipeline is not a separate orchestration framework. It is a governed Garden Process Algebra instance bound to canonical `REG-ALGEBRA-001`, `[S-PROCESS]#5.1`, and `[S-PROCESS]#5.1B`.\n+\n+Every update action must emit or reference a validated `GardenEvolutionAlgebraUsageReceipt/v1` and an `AlgebraBoundUpdateReceipt/v1`. These receipts describe operator use; they grant no authority and do not prove Garden certification.\n+\n+The Process operators are used as follows:\n+\n+- `SEQUENCE(A,B)` — ordered state transitions and gates.\n+- `PARALLEL({P_i})` — blind peer reviewers operating on one identical `packet_hash`.\n+- `COMPOSE(A,B)` — composition of validated stages without assuming extra algebra laws.\n+- `BRANCH(c,A,B)` — semantic-vs-LIGHT routing, pass-vs-repair routing, and target-kind routing.\n+- `RETRY(P,bound)` — bounded repair attempts.\n+- `ROLLBACK(P)` — reverse a failed governed integration or promoted successor when rollback policy permits.\n+- `COMPENSATE(P)` — remediate external side effects that cannot be erased by rollback.\n+- `RECONSTRUCT(x)` — rebuild process state only from bound evidence/receipts; reconstruction never recreates authority from untrusted history.\n+- `ITERATE(P,k)` — bounded continuous upgrade cycles.\n+- `RECURSE(P)` — nested dependency repair only under explicit termination/resource bounds.\n+- `TERMINATE(reason)` — stop on invalid authority, unresolved critical conflict, failed completeness, or exhausted safe retry bounds.\n+- `DO_NOTHING` — required valid result when no change has positive verified value.\n+\n+No associativity, commutativity, idempotence, distributivity or reordering law is inferred unless canonically registered. In particular, parallel reviewer effects are **not** assumed commutative.\n+\n+### One update process, three targets\n+\n+All updates use the same algebraic control flow:\n+\n+`Observe/Discover -> Packetize -> PARALLEL(blind review) -> Cross-Examine -> BRANCH(semantic, full semantic path, LIGHT path) -> Build/Repair -> Verify -> BRANCH(pass, target-specific integration, RETRY/ROLLBACK/TERMINATE) -> Post-Fix Verify -> ITERATE or DO_NOTHING`\n+\n+The target-specific terminal rule is the only major difference:\n+\n+1. `PUBLIC_REPO` — exact-head governed merge into the public repository.\n+2. `PRIVATE_IMPLEMENTATION` — exact-head governed merge into the private implementation repository.\n+3. `CANONICAL_DESIGN` — never edit the immutable predecessor; compose a complete successor candidate, then require separately protected human canonical promotion.\n+\n+Policy Algebra applies only where a registered authority/approval operator is actually executed. Ordinary ActionGate checks must not be silently relabeled as Policy Algebra.\n+\n+Conformance Algebra is required for requirement/reference/test/obligation closure and exact-head conformance. Decision and Evidence Algebra remain explicit `FRONTIER` where their executable operator signatures are not yet bound. Bridge Algebra is conditional and applies only when a registered semantic-to-representation transformation is actually invoked.\n+\n+Therefore Process v2 is **algebra-bound by construction**, but it must continue to report `semantic_compliance_proved=false` until every required frontier for the action is closed and a valid algebra validation receipt exists. `FRONTIER` never counts as compliance.\ndiff --git a/PROCESS_VERSION.json b/PROCESS_VERSION.json\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..354c2716d2d5b7a3f1bde046ea25fc8e5315e274\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/PROCESS_VERSION.json\n@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@\n+{\n+  \"schema\": \"GardenProcessVersion/v1\",\n+  \"process_version\": \"GardenProcess-v2.0-candidate\",\n+  \"status\": \"CANDIDATE_REVIEW_UNDER_PREDECESSOR\",\n+  \"predecessor_process\": \"GardenProcess-v1-current\",\n+  \"effective_only_after_admission\": true,\n+  \"self_authorization_forbidden\": true,\n+  \"canonical_garden_version\": \"v15.5\",\n+  \"canonical_garden_unchanged\": true,\n+  \"protected_surfaces\": [\n+    \"review_independence\",\n+    \"review_packet_completeness\",\n+    \"evidence_classification\",\n+    \"cycle_state_transitions\",\n+    \"merge_protocol\",\n+    \"freeze_rules\",\n+    \"repair_priority\",\n+    \"reviewer_allocation\",\n+    \"budget_and_escalation\",\n+    \"human_availability_and_authority\",\n+    \"semantic_admission\",\n+    \"canonical_promotion\",\n+    \"pipeline_health\",\n+    \"process_algebra_binding\"\n+  ],\n+  \"process_algebra_binding\": {\n+    \"registry_ref\": \"REG-ALGEBRA-001\",\n+    \"canonical_refs\": [\n+      \"Garden_System_v15.5:[S-PROCESS]#5.1\",\n+      \"Garden_System_v15.5:[S-PROCESS]#5.1B\"\n+    ],\n+    \"required\": true,\n+    \"semantic_compliance_proved\": false,\n+    \"rule\": \"Every governed update action must declare its registered Process Algebra operator composition and bind an algebra-usage receipt. Garden Continuous Upgrade Process v2 (candidate)\n\nStatus: protected process candidate. Review under the predecessor process. Canonical Garden v15.5 is unchanged.\n\n## Eight stages\n\n1. **Observe / Discover** — inspect repo state, incidents, failed CI, open repair obligations, design coverage and new public research. Research may emit `CANDIDATE_ALTERNATIVE`; it may not declare an alternative better.\n2. **Packetize** — build one coherent-complete `GardenReviewPacket/v1` for one immutable `CycleID`. Canonical packet bytes have one SHA-256 `packet_hash` used by every blind reviewer.\n3. **Blind Review** — all model/specialist reviewers see the same packet and no same-cycle peer findings. Same-base specialists add coverage, not independent-family count.\n4. **Cross-Examine** — starts only after required blind findings complete for the same CycleID/packet_hash. Evidence is checked, not voted on.\n5. **Build / Repair** — implement the smallest verified-value change. Non-semantic work may use LIGHT; semantic work uses DesignEpoch-bound deltas.\n6. **Verify** — independently prove the original failure is gone and no regression or semantic drift was introduced.\n7. **Successor / Promotion** — compose admitted deltas into a complete successor. Immutable predecessor remains untouched. Protected canonical promotion remains human-only.\n8. **Pipeline Health / Hygiene** — audit lane health, repair backlog, branch/PR state, stale evidence, budgets, model yield and process complexity.\n\n## Coherent-complete ReviewPacket\n\n`PacketCompletenessReceipt/v1` passes only when:\n- exact public/private repo commits in scope are bound, or inaccessibility is explicit;\n- canonical version, DesignEpoch/source root and ProcessVersion are bound;\n- the bounded target text, source hashes and anchors are included;\n- each repo has a defined base/head diff or mechanically proven `NO_CHANGE`;\n- changed files and mechanically derived changed symbols are included;\n- dependency/reference closure and owner definitions are mechanically derived;\n- affected schemas, FunctionContracts, invariants, tests, registries and implementation bindings are included;\n- exact-head CI/test/environment evidence is included;\n- active freezes and unresolved/reopened findings touching closure objects are linked;\n- every exclusion has a machine-checkable reason; and\n- the closure frontier shows that adding another object would leave the declared closure boundary.\n\nMissing required material, ambiguous closure, stale binding or unexplained exclusion => `PACKET_INCOMPLETE`.\n\n## State coherence\n\nClock schedules are watchdogs, not authority to advance. A cycle moves only through:\n\n`PACKET_BUILDING -> PACKET_READY -> BLIND_REVIEW -> BLIND_REVIEW_COMPLETE -> CROSS_EXAM -> BUILDER_READY -> INTEGRATED -> POST_FIX_VERIFIED`\n\nSlow cycles remain isolated. Adjacent CycleIDs may not mix evidence. If cycles accumulate, intake is throttled instead of dropping findings.\n\n## Evidence classes\n\nThe producer assigns `claimed_evidence_class` and attaches the evidence object. A deterministic validator checks whether the evidence can support that class. Once validated, the class is immutable. Reclassification requires `EvidenceClassChallenge` with same-or-higher-class evidence.\n\n- **E4** reproducible executable trace, bound to exact commit/DesignEpoch/environment/command/result.\n- **E3** formal proof or machine-checkable contradiction with current premise bindings.\n- **E2** authoritative exact source/code/schema evidence.\n- **E1** reasoned argument.\n- **E0** unsupported assertion.\n\nIntegrators may not silently relabel evidence through selective citation.\n\n## Exact-head merge protocol\n\nUse merge-queue / rebase-and-reverify semantics. If main moves after verification, the old exact-head evidence expires. Rebase/update onto current main, rebuild affected evidence, rerun required tests/checks, then merge. Never silently merge X-verified work onto Y. No force-push or gate bypass.\n\n## Repair obligations and aging\n\nEvery verified defect becomes a typed `RepairObligation` with severity, first-seen cycle, age, attempts, blocker, exact affected objects, reproduction evidence, expected repair and tests.\n\n- CRITICAL preempts ordinary upgrades.\n- HIGH gains priority with age.\n- MEDIUM also ages upward.\n- `REPAIR_BACKLOG_PRESSURE` throttles new discovery/feature intake when repair debt exceeds policy thresholds.\n\nOpening an issue, freezing work or getting model agreement is not a fix.\n\n## Reviewer allocation\n\nPrimary yield signals are external outcomes: seeded-defect detection, independently `VERIFIED_FIXED` results, reproducible counterexamples, incident/regression attribution and false-positive rate. Challenger/cross-exam acceptance is secondary and capped. Preserve a fixed exploration/diversity share.\n\n## Human availability\n\n- `HUMAN_AVAILABLE`: normal operation.\n- `HUMAN_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE`: protected promotion pauses; routine governed work continues; admitted-unpromoted semantic work is capped at one major-candidate batch (normally <=10 deltas unless already MAJOR). Beyond cap => `DEFERRED_PENDING_PROMOTION_CAPACITY`.\n- `HUMAN_PERMANENTLY_UNAVAILABLE`: canonical promotion remains frozen until a separately admitted succession/continuity mechanism exists. Keep the same cap; already-authorized safety/correctness implementation against the last valid DesignEpoch may continue; issue a 90-day continuity-status review.\n\nProtected authority is never silently transferred.\n\n## Budgets\n\nRoutine public OpenRouter review keeps the existing ~USD 1/day ceiling. Expensive escalation uses a separate `EscalationBudget` and defaults to zero automatic spend unless explicitly pre-authorized.\n\n## Process change control\n\nSemantic changes to review independence, packet completeness, evidence rules, state transitions, merge/freeze rules, budgets, human authority, admission/promotion, pipeline-health semantics, or Process Algebra binding require a ProcessVersion bump. ProcessVersion N+1 is reviewed under N and cannot self-authorize. Pure implementation fixes preserving admitted process semantics may use the non-semantic track.\n\n## Pipeline health\n\n`PipelineHealthReceipt/v1` records, per lane: last successful cycle, attempts/completions, latency, missed/stale cycles, backlog, failure reasons, unresolved repairs, independently verified closures, false closures, stale evidence, packet-incomplete rate and health state `HEALTHY|DEGRADED|REDUCED_OPERATION|FAILED`.\n\nNo lane certifies its own closure counts. Unhealthy lanes reduce operation or fail closed until repaired.\n\n## Simplification\n\nQuarterly `PipelineSimplificationReview/v1` looks for zero-yield lanes, duplicate gates, rules that never fire, record types with no consumers, obsolete paths and high-cost/low-value checks. Removal is a first-class improvement. Rare but load-bearing safety/privacy/authority controls are not removed merely because they rarely fire.\n\n## Garden Process Algebra binding\n\nThe upgrade pipeline is not a separate orchestration framework. It is a governed Garden Process Algebra instance bound to canonical `REG-ALGEBRA-001`, `[S-PROCESS]#5.1`, and `[S-PROCESS]#5.1B`.\n\nEvery update action must emit or reference a validated `GardenEvolutionAlgebraUsageReceipt/v1` and an `AlgebraBoundUpdateReceipt/v1`. These receipts describe operator use; they grant no authority and do not prove Garden certification.\n\nThe Process operators are used as follows:\n\n- `SEQUENCE(A,B)` — ordered state transitions and gates.\n- `PARALLEL({P_i})` — blind peer reviewers operating on one identical `packet_hash`.\n- `COMPOSE(A,B)` — composition of validated stages without assuming extra algebra laws.\n- `BRANCH(c,A,B)` — semantic-vs-LIGHT routing, pass-vs-repair routing, and target-kind routing.\n- `RETRY(P,bound)` — bounded repair attempts.\n- `ROLLBACK(P)` — reverse a failed governed integration or promoted successor when rollback policy permits.\n- `COMPENSATE(P)` — remediate external side effects that cannot be erased by rollback.\n- `RECONSTRUCT(x)` — rebuild process state only from bound evidence/receipts; reconstruction never recreates authority from untrusted history.\n- `ITERATE(P,k)` — bounded continuous upgrade cycles.\n- `RECURSE(P)` — nested dependency repair only under explicit termination/resource bounds.\n- `TERMINATE(reason)` — stop on invalid authority, unresolved critical conflict, failed completeness, or exhausted safe retry bounds.\n- `DO_NOTHING` — required valid result when no change has positive verified value.\n\nNo associativity, commutativity, idempotence, distributivity or reordering law is inferred unless canonically registered. In particular, parallel reviewer effects are **not** assumed commutative.\n\n### One update process, three targets\n\nAll updates use the same algebraic control flow:\n\n`Observe/Discover -> Packetize -> PARALLEL(blind review) -> Cross-Examine -> BRANCH(semantic, full semantic path, LIGHT path) -> Build/Repair -> Verify -> BRANCH(pass, target-specific integration, RETRY/ROLLBACK/TERMINATE) -> Post-Fix Verify -> ITERATE or DO_NOTHING`\n\nThe target-specific terminal rule is the only major difference:\n\n1. `PUBLIC_REPO` — exact-head governed merge into the public repository.\n2. `PRIVATE_IMPLEMENTATION` — exact-head governed merge into the private implementation repository.\n3. `CANONICAL_DESIGN` — never edit the immutable predecessor; compose a complete successor candidate, then require separately protected human canonical promotion.\n\nPolicy Algebra applies only where a registered authority/approval operator is actually executed. Ordinary ActionGate checks must not be silently relabeled as Policy Algebra.\n\nConformance Algebra is required for requirement/reference/test/obligation closure and exact-head conformance. Decision and Evidence Algebra remain explicit `FRONTIER` where their executable operator signatures are not yet bound. Bridge Algebra is conditional and applies only when a registered semantic-to-representation transformation is actually invoked.\n\nTherefore Process v2 is **algebra-bound by construction**, but it must continue to report `semantic_compliance_proved=false` until every required frontier for the action is closed and a valid algebra validation receipt exists. `FRONTIER` never counts as
